# Universal Taxonomy of Alchemies
**Version:** 0.14.0  
**Status:** Living taxonomy; version 0.14.0 includes the After-Death Power Continuity Alchemy expansion; cumulative records and evidence status are maintained by category; expandable and subject to evidence-based review  
**Canonical scope:** C2O, UNIVERSALIS, UNIVERSALINS, and UNIVERSALIN Guardians  
**Repository scope:** C2O-OS, C2O-App, UNIVERSALIS-OS, UNIVERSALIS-App

## Governing definition
An **Alchemy** is a named, bounded process or capability that transforms, combines, separates, preserves, interprets, regulates, protects, repairs, or otherwise changes a state, relationship, resource, system, or outcome. The term is used as a taxonomy label; it does not imply that unrelated disciplines are literally the same process.

## Taxonomy rules
1. This document is a **list-first taxonomy**. Every Alchemy must have a stable identifier, a distinct name, and a concise scope.
2. The twelve Zodiac stages are the organizing sequence. They are functional classification stages, not claims of scientific causation or astrological determinism.
3. An Alchemy may be cross-listed in multiple stages when its functions genuinely span them; retain one canonical identifier.
4. Every constructive Alchemy must be paired with relevant prevention, protection, detection, correction, recovery, or restoration Alchemies. Safety is part of the taxonomy, not an optional appendix.
5. Adversarial, harmful, deceptive, coercive, exploitative, or destructive patterns may be catalogued for recognition and defense only. Their inclusion is not endorsement or an instruction to cause harm.
6. Do not force a subject into an Alchemy when the transformation or capability is unclear. Record it as a candidate, define its boundaries, and validate it.
7. Distinguish established science and engineering from hypotheses, metaphors, proposed specifications, and fictional or symbolic interpretations.
8. C2O and UNIVERSALIS share the underlying OS and application architecture but retain separate private-source/company and public/open-source governance boundaries. This taxonomy is a shared public classification contract; it must not disclose private keys, private policies, confidential incident data, or other C2O-only material.
9. UNIVERSALINS are non-personal assistants. UNIVERSALIN Guardians are personal human assistants/guardians. Neither category creates additional top-level intelligences beyond the established C2O and UNIVERSALIS architecture.
10. New entries require a definition, scope, inputs, outputs, dependencies, risks, safeguards, evidence status, and links to related Alchemies.

## Zodiac Stage I — Aries: Initiation and Emergence
- **A001 — Origin Alchemy:** Identify and define an initial state, source, need, or originating condition.
- **A002 — Discovery Alchemy:** Find previously unknown entities, patterns, resources, questions, or opportunities.
- **A003 — Observation Alchemy:** Gather observations without prematurely converting them into conclusions.
- **A004 — Questioning Alchemy:** Convert uncertainty or curiosity into answerable questions.
- **A005 — Hypothesis Alchemy:** Form provisional, testable explanations or models.
- **A006 — Ideation Alchemy:** Generate candidate concepts, designs, and possible transformations.
- **A007 — Opportunity Alchemy:** Identify viable possibilities, constraints, and potential value.
- **A008 — Boundary-Setting Alchemy:** Define scope, assumptions, exclusions, and decision limits before action.
- **A009 — Consent and Authorization Alchemy:** Establish legitimate permission, ownership, and authority before initiating consequential action.
- **A010 — Anti-Coercion Alchemy:** Prevent forced participation, unauthorized initiation, manipulation, and action without valid consent.

## Zodiac Stage II — Taurus: Foundation and Materialization
- **A011 — Elemental Alchemy:** Study and transform elemental and material states within their actual scientific domains.
- **A012 — Material Alchemy:** Transform, process, join, separate, and characterize materials.
- **A013 — Chemical Alchemy:** Describe chemical reactions, synthesis, decomposition, and molecular change.
- **A014 — Molecular Alchemy:** Model molecular structures, interactions, and transformations.
- **A015 — Geological Alchemy:** Study formation, alteration, cycling, and stabilization of geological materials.
- **A016 — Biological Foundation Alchemy:** Study the material and biochemical foundations of living systems.
- **A017 — Resource Alchemy:** Allocate, conserve, recover, and responsibly reuse resources.
- **A018 — Infrastructure Alchemy:** Establish dependable physical, digital, and organizational foundations.
- **A019 — Quality and Provenance Alchemy:** Preserve traceability, authenticity, composition, and quality information.
- **A020 — Anti-Degradation Alchemy:** Prevent contamination, corrosion, decay, material failure, and avoidable resource loss.

## Zodiac Stage III — Gemini: Connection and Communication
- **A021 — Relational Alchemy:** Identify, model, and transform relationships among entities.
- **A022 — Connection Alchemy:** Establish valid connections among components, systems, people, and knowledge.
- **A023 — Communication Alchemy:** Encode, transmit, receive, and interpret information.
- **A024 — Language Alchemy:** Transform meaning across languages, vocabularies, symbols, and representational systems.
- **A025 — Translation Alchemy:** Preserve intended meaning while changing language, format, or medium.
- **A026 — Interoperability Alchemy:** Enable distinct systems to exchange information and coordinate through defined contracts.
- **A027 — Knowledge-Linking Alchemy:** Connect claims, evidence, sources, concepts, and dependencies.
- **A028 — Collaboration Alchemy:** Coordinate contributions while preserving roles, accountability, and independent judgment.
- **A029 — Miscommunication-Detection Alchemy:** Detect ambiguity, distortion, context loss, and conflicting interpretations.
- **A030 — Anti-Deception Alchemy:** Detect and resist impersonation, misinformation, forged provenance, deceptive framing, and unauthorized influence.

## Zodiac Stage IV — Cancer: Care, Continuity, and Protection
- **A031 — Preservation Alchemy:** Maintain integrity and continuity of valuable states, records, resources, and capabilities.
- **A032 — Protection Alchemy:** Reduce exposure to harm through proportionate safeguards.
- **A033 — Privacy Alchemy:** Minimize, compartmentalize, govern, and protect personal and confidential information.
- **A034 — Security Alchemy:** Protect systems, identities, assets, and operations against unauthorized access or misuse.
- **A035 — Safety Alchemy:** Identify hazards and control risks to people, systems, and environments.
- **A036 — Resilience Alchemy:** Maintain essential functions under disruption and degraded conditions.
- **A037 — Backup and Recovery Alchemy:** Create and validate recoverable copies and restoration procedures.
- **A038 — Care Alchemy:** Support human needs, dignity, accessibility, and wellbeing without overriding autonomy.
- **A039 — Safeguarding Alchemy:** Protect vulnerable people, dependent systems, and entrusted resources from exploitation or neglect.
- **A040 — Anti-Exploitation Alchemy:** Prevent abuse of trust, dependency, access, power, data, or resource asymmetry.

## Zodiac Stage V — Leo: Expression and Manifestation
- **A041 — Creative Alchemy:** Transform ideas and experiences into original expressive work.
- **A042 — Constructive Alchemy:** Turn requirements, resources, and designs into functioning things.
- **A043 — Design Alchemy:** Convert needs and constraints into coherent structures and experiences.
- **A044 — Engineering Alchemy:** Apply disciplined methods to create, test, and maintain useful systems.
- **A045 — Software Alchemy:** Transform specifications and algorithms into reliable software.
- **A046 — Interface Alchemy:** Translate system capabilities into understandable, accessible human interactions.
- **A047 — Automation Alchemy:** Delegate repeatable operations to controlled mechanisms with observability and override.
- **A048 — Deployment Alchemy:** Release validated changes into an intended environment under controlled conditions.
- **A049 — Attribution Alchemy:** Preserve authorship, contribution, licensing, and provenance.
- **A050 — Anti-Manipulation Alchemy:** Prevent dark patterns, deceptive interfaces, coercive persuasion, and hidden consequential behavior.

## Zodiac Stage VI — Virgo: Analysis, Refinement, and Verification
- **A051 — Analytical Alchemy:** Decompose complex subjects into inspectable parts and relationships.
- **A052 — Measurement Alchemy:** Quantify states and changes with calibrated methods and stated uncertainty.
- **A053 — Mathematical Alchemy:** Represent and transform problems using valid mathematical structures.
- **A054 — Computational Alchemy:** Execute models, algorithms, and reproducible computations.
- **A055 — Scientific Alchemy:** Develop knowledge through observation, experimentation, falsifiability, and replication.
- **A056 — Research Alchemy:** Convert questions into protocols, evidence, findings, and bounded conclusions.
- **A057 — Testing Alchemy:** Evaluate expected and unexpected behavior against explicit criteria.
- **A058 — Verification Alchemy:** Independently confirm that specified properties or claims hold.
- **A059 — Validation Alchemy:** Determine whether a result is fit for its intended use and context.
- **A060 — Error-Correction Alchemy:** Detect, localize, correct, and document errors without concealing their history.

## Zodiac Stage VII — Libra: Balance, Ethics, and Governance
- **A061 — Balance Alchemy:** Reconcile competing requirements without erasing legitimate differences.
- **A062 — Ethical Alchemy:** Evaluate actions against explicit principles, duties, rights, and foreseeable consequences.
- **A063 — Justice Alchemy:** Identify and address unfair treatment, inequitable impact, and unjust process.
- **A064 — Governance Alchemy:** Define legitimate authority, decision rights, accountability, and oversight.
- **A065 — Policy Alchemy:** Translate principles and obligations into clear, testable rules.
- **A066 — Consent-Respecting Alchemy:** Preserve meaningful choice, revocability, and informed participation.
- **A067 — Conflict-Resolution Alchemy:** Resolve disputes through transparent procedures and proportionate remedies.
- **A068 — Resource-Fairness Alchemy:** Allocate access and resources using explicit, reviewable criteria.
- **A069 — Auditability Alchemy:** Make consequential processes traceable and independently reviewable.
- **A070 — Anti-Corruption Alchemy:** Prevent bribery, conflicts of interest, capture, favoritism, abuse of authority, and concealed self-dealing.

## Zodiac Stage VIII — Scorpio: Deep Transformation and Adversarial Resistance
- **A071 — Transformative Alchemy:** Produce and evaluate substantial changes in state, structure, capability, or relationship.
- **A072 — Decomposition Alchemy:** Separate a complex whole into constituent parts for analysis or safe processing.
- **A073 — Integration Alchemy:** Combine compatible components while preserving boundaries and provenance.
- **A074 — Adaptation Alchemy:** Adjust behavior or structure in response to changing conditions.
- **A075 — Metamorphic Alchemy:** Track and govern multi-stage changes while preserving required invariants.
- **A076 — Threat-Modeling Alchemy:** Identify assets, adversaries, attack surfaces, trust boundaries, and plausible harms.
- **A077 — Adversarial-Testing Alchemy:** Safely challenge systems to expose weaknesses before real-world exploitation.
- **A078 — Abuse-Pattern Recognition Alchemy:** Recognize harmful patterns such as coercion, fraud, sabotage, exploitation, and destructive misuse.
- **A079 — Containment Alchemy:** Limit the spread and impact of failures, compromise, or harmful behavior.
- **A080 — Anti-Destruction Alchemy:** Prevent sabotage, malicious tampering, uncontrolled cascading failure, and irreversible damage.

## Zodiac Stage IX — Sagittarius: Expansion, Learning, and Exploration
- **A081 — Learning Alchemy:** Convert experience, instruction, and evidence into improved understanding or capability.
- **A082 — Teaching Alchemy:** Structure knowledge so others can understand, practice, and evaluate it.
- **A083 — Exploration Alchemy:** Investigate unfamiliar domains while tracking uncertainty and risk.
- **A084 — Interdisciplinary Alchemy:** Combine methods from distinct fields without collapsing their standards or meanings.
- **A085 — Scaling Alchemy:** Extend a capability while measuring new costs, risks, and failure modes.
- **A086 — Generalization Alchemy:** Identify which principles transfer across contexts and which remain context-specific.
- **A087 — Accessibility Alchemy:** Remove avoidable barriers to participation, comprehension, and use.
- **A088 — Cultural-Context Alchemy:** Adapt communication and systems to context without stereotyping or erasing difference.
- **A089 — Future-Scenario Alchemy:** Explore plausible futures as scenarios, not as established predictions.
- **A090 — Anti-Overreach Alchemy:** Prevent unsupported certainty, scope creep, unsafe scaling, and claims beyond available evidence.

## Zodiac Stage X — Capricorn: Structure, Operations, and Accountability
- **A091 — Structural Alchemy:** Organize components, roles, dependencies, and constraints into durable structures.
- **A092 — Operational Alchemy:** Convert plans into controlled, observable, repeatable operations.
- **A093 — Process Alchemy:** Define workflows, handoffs, checkpoints, and completion criteria.
- **A094 — Reliability Alchemy:** Improve consistency, availability, fault tolerance, and predictable behavior.
- **A095 — Performance Alchemy:** Optimize measurable performance without violating safety or correctness requirements.
- **A096 — Energy Alchemy:** Measure and responsibly optimize energy use and energy-related trade-offs.
- **A097 — Economic Alchemy:** Analyze value, cost, incentives, exchange, and resource sustainability.
- **A098 — Documentation Alchemy:** Preserve operational knowledge, decisions, assumptions, and change history.
- **A099 — Change-Control Alchemy:** Review, authorize, stage, and track consequential changes.
- **A100 — Anti-Brittleness Alchemy:** Prevent single points of failure, hidden dependencies, unmaintainable complexity, and uncontrolled change.

## Zodiac Stage XI — Aquarius: Systems, Networks, and Collective Intelligence
- **A101 — Systems Alchemy:** Model whole systems, feedback loops, boundaries, and emergent behavior.
- **A102 — Network Alchemy:** Design and govern connected nodes, flows, trust, and dependencies.
- **A103 — Data Alchemy:** Collect, clean, structure, transform, govern, and interpret data.
- **A104 — Information Alchemy:** Convert data into contextualized information while preserving uncertainty and provenance.
- **A105 — Knowledge Alchemy:** Organize evidence-supported knowledge for retrieval, reasoning, and reuse.
- **A106 — Intelligence-Support Alchemy:** Provide bounded analytical and operational support while retaining human accountability.
- **A107 — UNIVERSALINS Support Alchemy:** Enable non-personal UNIVERSALINS to support humans, the technological grid, both OS editions, both application editions, and the wider world within explicit permissions.
- **A108 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Support Alchemy:** Support personal human assistance and guardianship with consent, privacy, user control, and clear separation from non-personal UNIVERSALINS.
- **A109 — Distributed-Coordination Alchemy:** Coordinate distributed components without assuming centralized control is always appropriate.
- **A110 — Anti-Monopoly-of-Control Alchemy:** Prevent hidden centralization, unauthorized privilege escalation, lock-in, and concentration of decision power.

## Zodiac Stage XII — Pisces: Completion, Restoration, and Renewal
- **A111 — Completion Alchemy:** Determine whether defined objectives and acceptance criteria have been met.
- **A112 — Synthesis Alchemy:** Produce a coherent account from compatible findings while preserving disagreement and uncertainty.
- **A113 — Restoration Alchemy:** Return damaged or disrupted systems and relationships to a safe, intended condition where possible.
- **A114 — Repair Alchemy:** Remedy identified defects and verify that the remedy works.
- **A115 — Reconciliation Alchemy:** Restore workable relationships, records, or states after conflict or inconsistency.
- **A116 — Renewal Alchemy:** Retire, replace, or regenerate components responsibly.
- **A117 — Decommissioning Alchemy:** End systems and processes safely, with data, access, and dependency handling.
- **A118 — Remediation Alchemy:** Address root causes, residual risks, and affected parties after an incident or failure.
- **A119 — Lessons-Learned Alchemy:** Convert outcomes and failures into documented, testable improvements.
- **A120 — Anti-Recurrence Alchemy:** Verify that corrective controls reduce the likelihood or impact of repeated harm.

## Cross-stage preventative and defensive Alchemies
- **A121 — Prevention Alchemy:** Stop foreseeable harm before it occurs through proportionate, evidence-based controls.
- **A122 — Early-Warning Alchemy:** Detect leading indicators and escalating risk before thresholds are crossed.
- **A123 — Detection Alchemy:** Identify harmful, anomalous, unauthorized, or unsafe conditions.
- **A124 — Defense-in-Depth Alchemy:** Apply independent, layered safeguards so one failure does not defeat all protection.
- **A125 — Least-Privilege Alchemy:** Grant only the minimum permissions required for a defined task and duration.
- **A126 — Isolation Alchemy:** Separate trust domains, workloads, identities, data, and failure zones.
- **A127 — Secure-Default Alchemy:** Make the safer configuration the default and require explicit authorization for elevated risk.
- **A128 — Human-Oversight Alchemy:** Ensure consequential or high-impact actions have meaningful, informed, and appropriately placed human oversight.
- **A129 — Reversibility Alchemy:** Prefer changes that can be paused, rolled back, or safely undone.
- **A130 — Non-Maleficence Alchemy:** Avoid causing foreseeable, unjustified harm and stop when safe operation cannot be maintained.
- **A131 — Truthfulness Alchemy:** Prevent fabrication, false provenance, concealed uncertainty, and unsupported claims.
- **A132 — Evidence-Integrity Alchemy:** Protect records from tampering, selective omission, and misleading presentation.
- **A133 — Supply-Chain Integrity Alchemy:** Verify origins, dependencies, builds, updates, and attestations.
- **A134 — Incident-Response Alchemy:** Prepare, detect, triage, contain, investigate, recover, and learn from incidents.
- **A135 — Continuity Alchemy:** Maintain essential services and safe degradation during disruption.
- **A136 — Harm-Reduction Alchemy:** Reduce severity and exposure when a risk cannot be fully eliminated.
- **A137 — Accountability Alchemy:** Assign responsibility, preserve decision records, and provide review and remedy.
- **A138 — Whistleblowing and Escalation Alchemy:** Provide protected routes to report serious risks, misconduct, and unresolved safety concerns.
- **A139 — Environmental-Stewardship Alchemy:** Prevent avoidable ecological damage and account for lifecycle impacts.
- **A140 — Exit-and-Deletion Alchemy:** Support safe disengagement, data export, access revocation, and verified deletion where appropriate.

## Adversarial and degenerative patterns — recognition and prevention only
These are **harm-pattern classifications**, not constructive capabilities to implement. Each must link to one or more defensive Alchemies above.
- **X001 — Coercion Pattern:** Forced action or participation; counter with A009, A010, A066, A128.
- **X002 — Deception Pattern:** Deliberate misrepresentation or forged identity/provenance; counter with A030, A049, A131, A132.
- **X003 — Exploitation Pattern:** Taking unfair advantage of vulnerability, access, or dependency; counter with A038, A039, A040, A137.
- **X004 — Privacy-Invasion Pattern:** Unauthorized collection, exposure, inference, or retention; counter with A033, A125, A126, A140.
- **X005 — Sabotage Pattern:** Deliberate damage or disruption; counter with A076, A079, A080, A134.
- **X006 — Manipulation Pattern:** Hidden influence that undermines informed choice; counter with A050, A062, A066, A131.
- **X007 — Corruption Pattern:** Abuse of entrusted authority for improper benefit; counter with A064, A069, A070, A137.
- **X008 — Discrimination Pattern:** Unjust differential treatment or exclusion; counter with A063, A068, A087, A088.
- **X009 — Unsafe-Autonomy Pattern:** Consequential action beyond granted authority or without adequate oversight; counter with A008, A009, A047, A128.
- **X010 — Evidence-Tampering Pattern:** Alteration, concealment, or fabrication of records; counter with A049, A069, A132.
- **X011 — Resource-Depletion Pattern:** Wasteful or extractive use that undermines future needs; counter with A017, A020, A096, A139.
- **X012 — Irreversibility Trap:** Unreviewable or destructive action without recovery path; counter with A037, A079, A099, A129.
- **X013 — Centralization-Abuse Pattern:** Concentration of unchecked control; counter with A064, A069, A109, A110.
- **X014 — Recurrence Pattern:** Repeated failure because root causes remain unaddressed; counter with A060, A118, A119, A120.

## Required record format for every future Alchemy
- **Identifier and name:**
- **Zodiac stage(s):**
- **Definition:**
- **Purpose and intended outcomes:**
- **Domain and scope:**
- **Inputs and prerequisites:**
- **Process / mechanism:**
- **Outputs and observable effects:**
- **Dependencies and relationships:**
- **Constructive use cases:**
- **Misuse, failure, and harm patterns:**
- **Prevention and protection Alchemies:**
- **Detection, correction, recovery, and restoration Alchemies:**
- **Authority, consent, and human-oversight requirements:**
- **Privacy and security requirements:**
- **Evidence status:** proposed / documented / implemented / tested / verified / unverified / failed / unresolved
- **Sources and verification method:**
- **Limitations and open questions:**
- **Change history:**

## Cross-repository maintenance rules
- Keep this canonical taxonomy file synchronized across all four repositories.
- Preserve identical taxonomy identifiers and definitions in all four copies.
- Repository-specific implementation notes may be added separately; they must not silently fork the taxonomy.
- A proposed entry is not automatically an implemented feature, verified capability, scientific fact, or programme credit.
- Before adding an Alchemy, check for duplicates, define its distinct transformation, map its Zodiac stage(s), identify its harmful counterpart or misuse risks, and link prevention/protection/correction/restoration entries.
- When an entry changes, record the version, rationale, evidence, affected repositories, and verification result.
- Never expose C2O private-source secrets or confidential operational material through the shared taxonomy.


# Body Alchemy — Domain Expansion
**Originating Alchemy:** Body Alchemy  
**Family version:** 0.1.0  
**Zodiac stages:** All twelve; each entry below has a primary stage and may be cross-listed where justified.  
**Scope:** Human bodies and, where biologically applicable, other living organisms. Human-specific entries must not be generalized to other species without evidence.

## Definition and governing principles
**Body Alchemy** is the organized study and classification of the body's structures, living processes, functions, interactions, capacities, variations, maintenance, protection, adaptation, and changes across the lifespan. It connects anatomy, physiology, cell and molecular biology, biochemistry, neuroscience, biomechanics, immunology, development, ecology, and other relevant disciplines without collapsing their distinct methods or evidence standards.

Body Alchemy is a taxonomy, not a medical diagnosis or treatment protocol. It does not imply that all bodies are the same, that every biological variation is a defect, or that a process can safely be altered. Medical and biological claims require appropriate evidence, context, and qualified review.

## Body Alchemy list

### Zodiac I — Aries: Origin, identity, and emergence
- **A141 — Body Identity Alchemy:** Describe an organism's bodily identity and distinguish it from assumptions based on appearance.
- **A142 — Biological Origin Alchemy:** Study the biological origins and initial formation of an organism.
- **A143 — Body Blueprint Alchemy:** Study genetic, epigenetic, developmental, and environmental contributions to bodily formation without treating any one factor as destiny.
- **A144 — Cellular Origin Alchemy:** Classify cell origins, lineages, differentiation, and renewal.
- **A145 — Embryonic Development Alchemy:** Study early developmental processes and their biological variation.
- **A146 — Body Boundary Alchemy:** Identify bodily boundaries, interfaces, and exchanges with the surrounding environment.
- **A147 — Body Awareness Alchemy:** Study awareness of bodily state, internal signals, and bodily experience.
- **A148 — Bodily Autonomy Alchemy:** Protect a person's authority over decisions concerning their own body, subject to applicable law and emergency safeguards.
- **A149 — Biological Variation Alchemy:** Describe natural variation without automatically classifying difference as pathology.
- **A150 — Anti-Bodily-Coercion Alchemy:** Prevent non-consensual bodily intervention, coercion, exploitation, and disregard for bodily autonomy.

### Zodiac II — Taurus: Structure, matter, and foundations
- **A151 — Anatomical Alchemy:** Study the body's structures, organization, location, and relationships.
- **A152 — Cellular Alchemy:** Study cells, their structures, functions, interactions, and life cycles.
- **A153 — Tissue Alchemy:** Study tissue types, organization, function, and maintenance.
- **A154 — Organ Alchemy:** Study individual organs, their structure, function, and integration.
- **A155 — Skeletal Alchemy:** Study bones, joints, cartilage, support, protection, and skeletal adaptation.
- **A156 — Muscular Alchemy:** Study skeletal, smooth, and cardiac muscle structure and function.
- **A157 — Connective-Tissue Alchemy:** Study fascia, tendons, ligaments, and other connective structures.
- **A158 — Skin and Barrier Alchemy:** Study skin and other protective interfaces, including barrier function.
- **A159 — Body Composition Alchemy:** Study the proportions and distribution of bodily tissues and components with appropriate measurement limitations.
- **A160 — Anti-Structural-Injury Alchemy:** Prevent avoidable damage to bones, joints, muscles, tissues, and protective barriers.

### Zodiac III — Gemini: Signals, sensation, and communication
- **A161 — Nervous-System Alchemy:** Study central, peripheral, and autonomic nervous-system structures and functions.
- **A162 — Neural-Signaling Alchemy:** Study electrical and chemical signaling among neurons and other excitable cells.
- **A163 — Sensory Alchemy:** Study sensory detection and processing, including sight, hearing, smell, taste, touch, balance, and internal sensation.
- **A164 — Proprioceptive Alchemy:** Study awareness of body position, movement, and force.
- **A165 — Interoceptive Alchemy:** Study perception of internal bodily states and signals.
- **A166 — Motor-Control Alchemy:** Study planning, coordination, execution, and regulation of movement.
- **A167 — Neuroplasticity Alchemy:** Study experience-related changes in nervous-system structure and function.
- **A168 — Neuroendocrine Communication Alchemy:** Study interactions between neural and hormonal signaling.
- **A169 — Body-Expression Alchemy:** Study communication through posture, movement, facial expression, voice, and other bodily signals.
- **A170 — Anti-Signal-Disruption Alchemy:** Prevent, detect, and mitigate avoidable interference with sensory, neural, and motor function.

### Zodiac IV — Cancer: Regulation, defense, and care
- **A171 — Immune-System Alchemy:** Study immune recognition, response, regulation, and memory.
- **A172 — Inflammatory-Response Alchemy:** Study inflammation as a regulated biological response and distinguish protective processes from harmful dysregulation.
- **A173 — Lymphatic Alchemy:** Study lymphatic circulation, fluid balance, and immune functions.
- **A174 — Microbiome Alchemy:** Study microbial communities and their interactions with the body and environment.
- **A175 — Barrier-Defense Alchemy:** Study physical, chemical, and biological barriers against environmental threats.
- **A176 — Homeostatic Alchemy:** Study mechanisms that maintain internal conditions within viable ranges.
- **A177 — Thermoregulatory Alchemy:** Study body-temperature regulation and heat exchange.
- **A178 — Fluid-and-Electrolyte Alchemy:** Study bodily fluid compartments and electrolyte regulation.
- **A179 — Pain-and-Protective-Response Alchemy:** Study pain and related protective responses while recognizing that pain experience is individual and complex.
- **A180 — Anti-Infection and Anti-Injury Alchemy:** Prevent avoidable infection and injury through evidence-based hygiene, environmental controls, protective equipment, and appropriate care.

### Zodiac V — Leo: Function, movement, and expression
- **A181 — Cardiovascular Alchemy:** Study the heart, blood, vessels, circulation, and transport functions.
- **A182 — Respiratory Alchemy:** Study ventilation, gas exchange, and respiratory regulation.
- **A183 — Digestive Alchemy:** Study ingestion, digestion, absorption, and elimination.
- **A184 — Metabolic Alchemy:** Study biochemical pathways that transform matter and energy in living systems.
- **A185 — Musculoskeletal-Movement Alchemy:** Study the integrated mechanics of muscles, bones, joints, and movement.
- **A186 — Vocal Alchemy:** Study respiration, phonation, resonance, articulation, and voice.
- **A187 — Reproductive-System Alchemy:** Study reproductive anatomy and physiology with respect for privacy, autonomy, and individual variation.
- **A188 — Urinary Alchemy:** Study kidney function, filtration, fluid regulation, and urinary processes.
- **A189 — Endocrine Alchemy:** Study hormone-producing organs, hormonal signaling, and endocrine regulation.
- **A190 — Anti-Functional-Overload Alchemy:** Prevent avoidable strain and unsafe demands on bodily systems; recognize that capacity varies by individual and context.

### Zodiac VI — Virgo: Measurement, diagnosis support, and refinement
- **A191 — Physiological Measurement Alchemy:** Study and measure bodily functions using valid methods and stated uncertainty.
- **A192 — Biomarker Alchemy:** Study biological indicators and their limits, context, and interpretation.
- **A193 — Clinical-Observation Alchemy:** Organize observations relevant to health assessment without treating observations alone as a diagnosis.
- **A194 — Body-Imaging Alchemy:** Classify imaging methods and the anatomical or physiological information they can provide.
- **A195 — Laboratory-Biology Alchemy:** Study analysis of bodily fluids, cells, tissues, and molecular markers.
- **A196 — Functional-Capacity Alchemy:** Assess bodily function in relation to activities, context, accessibility, and individual goals.
- **A197 — Biological-Rhythm Alchemy:** Study circadian, ultradian, seasonal, and other biological rhythms.
- **A198 — Recovery-Monitoring Alchemy:** Track recovery and changing function using appropriate, non-overstated measures.
- **A199 — Differential-Interpretation Alchemy:** Preserve alternative explanations and uncertainty when interpreting bodily signs or measurements.
- **A200 — Anti-Misdiagnosis Alchemy:** Reduce diagnostic error, bias, premature closure, inappropriate generalization, and overconfidence.

### Zodiac VII — Libra: Balance, integration, and individual needs
- **A201 — Organ-System Integration Alchemy:** Study how organ systems coordinate and depend on one another.
- **A202 — Neuroendocrine-Immune Integration Alchemy:** Study interactions among nervous, endocrine, and immune functions.
- **A203 — Cardiorespiratory Integration Alchemy:** Study coordinated cardiovascular and respiratory function.
- **A204 — Digestive-Metabolic Integration Alchemy:** Study the relationships among digestion, nutrient handling, metabolism, and energy balance.
- **A205 — Sleep-Wake Balance Alchemy:** Study sleep, wakefulness, recovery, and their regulatory systems.
- **A206 — Nutrition Alchemy:** Study nutrient requirements, intake, digestion, absorption, and nutritional context without prescribing a universal diet.
- **A207 — Hydration Alchemy:** Study water balance and context-dependent hydration needs.
- **A208 — Body-Adaptation Balance Alchemy:** Study how bodily systems adjust to internal and external demands while identifying limits and risks.
- **A209 — Disability and Accessibility Alchemy:** Understand bodily variation, disability, assistive needs, environmental barriers, and accessible participation without framing disabled bodies as lesser.
- **A210 — Anti-Imbalance Alchemy:** Identify and reduce avoidable dysregulation while avoiding simplistic assumptions that every bodily variable has one ideal value.

### Zodiac VIII — Scorpio: Change, repair, and deep biological processes
- **A211 — Cellular-Repair Alchemy:** Study cellular repair mechanisms and their limitations.
- **A212 — Tissue-Repair Alchemy:** Study tissue healing, remodeling, and scar formation.
- **A213 — Regeneration Alchemy:** Study replacement or regrowth of cells, tissues, and structures where biologically possible.
- **A214 — Wound-Healing Alchemy:** Study the stages and factors involved in wound healing.
- **A215 — Bone-Remodeling Alchemy:** Study bone formation, resorption, and adaptation.
- **A216 — Muscle-Adaptation Alchemy:** Study muscle response to loading, disuse, aging, and recovery.
- **A217 — Apoptosis and Cell-Turnover Alchemy:** Study regulated cell death and cellular replacement.
- **A218 — Genetic- and Epigenetic-Change Alchemy:** Study inherited and acquired changes in genetic regulation and their consequences.
- **A219 — Aging-Process Alchemy:** Study biological changes across the lifespan without reducing aging to a single mechanism.
- **A220 — Anti-Dysregulation Alchemy:** Prevent or mitigate harmful disruption of repair, growth, immune, metabolic, and cellular-control processes.

### Zodiac IX — Sagittarius: Development, capability, and adaptation
- **A221 — Lifespan-Development Alchemy:** Study bodily development from prenatal stages through later life.
- **A222 — Growth Alchemy:** Study changes in body size, proportions, tissues, and functional capacity.
- **A223 — Motor-Learning Alchemy:** Study acquisition and refinement of movement skills.
- **A224 — Physical-Conditioning Alchemy:** Study adaptations associated with physical activity, while accounting for individual health and capacity.
- **A225 — Endurance Alchemy:** Study the body's capacity to sustain activity and recover.
- **A226 — Strength Alchemy:** Study force production and its biological and biomechanical foundations.
- **A227 — Flexibility-and-Mobility Alchemy:** Study range of motion, tissue properties, joint function, and movement options.
- **A228 — Environmental-Adaptation Alchemy:** Study bodily responses to temperature, altitude, pressure, light, and other environmental conditions.
- **A229 — Acclimatization Alchemy:** Study gradual physiological adjustment to changed environmental conditions.
- **A230 — Anti-Overtraining and Overexposure Alchemy:** Prevent harmful loading, inadequate recovery, unsafe exposure, and unsupported performance demands.

### Zodiac X — Capricorn: Maintenance, performance, and lifecycle stewardship
- **A231 — Body-Maintenance Alchemy:** Classify routine practices and systems that support bodily function and care.
- **A232 — Preventive-Health Alchemy:** Study evidence-based prevention, screening, and risk reduction appropriate to individual context.
- **A233 — Ergonomic Alchemy:** Adapt tools, tasks, and environments to bodily needs and capabilities.
- **A234 — Posture-and-Load Alchemy:** Study posture, load distribution, and movement demands without prescribing one rigid ideal posture.
- **A235 — Occupational-Body Alchemy:** Study bodily effects of work, repetitive tasks, exposures, and workplace design.
- **A236 — Rest-and-Recovery Alchemy:** Study rest, recovery, and their roles in bodily adaptation and function.
- **A237 — Energy-Expenditure Alchemy:** Study bodily energy use across rest, movement, thermoregulation, and other processes.
- **A238 — Environmental-Health Alchemy:** Study effects of air, water, housing, pollutants, climate, and built environments on bodily health.
- **A239 — Assistive-Technology Alchemy:** Classify technologies that support mobility, communication, sensory access, daily activities, and bodily function.
- **A240 — Anti-Neglect Alchemy:** Prevent avoidable neglect of bodily needs, unsafe working conditions, inaccessible environments, and delayed attention to concerning changes.

### Zodiac XI — Aquarius: Body systems, technology, and collective knowledge
- **A241 — Systems-Physiology Alchemy:** Model the body as a network of interacting systems with feedback and constraints.
- **A242 — Biomechanical Alchemy:** Study forces, motion, leverage, balance, and mechanical properties of living bodies.
- **A243 — Bioelectrical Alchemy:** Study electrical activity in cells, nerves, muscles, and organs.
- **A244 — Biochemical-Network Alchemy:** Study interacting biochemical pathways and regulatory networks.
- **A245 — Bioinformatics Alchemy:** Apply computational methods to biological data with attention to provenance, bias, and privacy.
- **A246 — Human-Technology Interface Alchemy:** Study interactions between bodies and devices, interfaces, wearables, and assistive systems.
- **A247 — Prosthetic and Orthotic Alchemy:** Study devices and methods that support or replace aspects of bodily structure or function.
- **A248 — Rehabilitation Alchemy:** Classify multidisciplinary approaches that support function, participation, and personally meaningful goals.
- **A249 — Population-Health Alchemy:** Study patterns of health and bodily outcomes across populations while protecting privacy and avoiding ecological fallacies.
- **A250 — Anti-Biometric-Abuse Alchemy:** Prevent unauthorized bodily surveillance, biometric discrimination, non-consensual inference, and misuse of physiological data.

### Zodiac XII — Pisces: Restoration, continuity, and end-of-life dignity
- **A251 — Functional-Restoration Alchemy:** Support recovery of bodily function where feasible and desired.
- **A252 — Rehabilitation-and-Relearning Alchemy:** Study recovery of skills and adaptation after illness, injury, or changes in function.
- **A253 — Pain-Management Research Alchemy:** Classify evidence and approaches concerning pain relief, function, and quality of life; individual care decisions require qualified assessment.
- **A254 — Chronic-Condition Support Alchemy:** Organize long-term support, monitoring, accessibility, and self-management knowledge without defining a person by a condition.
- **A255 — Palliative-Care Alchemy:** Study approaches that relieve suffering and support quality of life in serious illness.
- **A256 — End-of-Life Dignity Alchemy:** Protect dignity, informed preferences, comfort, and respectful care at the end of life.
- **A257 — Body-Donation and Legacy Alchemy:** Classify consent-based donation, research, teaching, and legacy processes.
- **A258 — Biological-Data Continuity Alchemy:** Preserve, transfer, correct, or delete personal biological records according to consent, law, and legitimate retention requirements.
- **A259 — Post-Injury and Post-Illness Adaptation Alchemy:** Support safe adaptation to changed bodily function and environments.
- **A260 — Anti-Recurrence and Restoration Alchemy:** Learn from injury, illness, or system failure and reduce preventable recurrence while avoiding blame toward affected people.

## Body Alchemy — Preventative, protective, and corrective safeguards
- **A261 — Bodily-Autonomy Protection Alchemy:** Prevent non-consensual examination, treatment, experimentation, or bodily intervention.
- **A262 — Informed-Consent Alchemy:** Support understandable, voluntary, context-appropriate consent and the ability to ask questions or withdraw where applicable.
- **A263 — Infection-Prevention Alchemy:** Reduce infection risks through evidence-based hygiene, sanitation, vaccination where appropriate, and infection-control practices.
- **A264 — Injury-Prevention Alchemy:** Identify hazards and reduce preventable physical injury in daily life, work, sport, and care settings.
- **A265 — Toxic-Exposure Prevention Alchemy:** Identify and reduce harmful exposure to chemicals, pollutants, and other toxic agents.
- **A266 — Medication-Safety Alchemy:** Reduce medication errors, unsafe interactions, inappropriate use, and failures of communication through qualified medication review and reliable processes.
- **A267 — Nutrition-Harm Prevention Alchemy:** Prevent malnutrition, unsafe restriction, disordered-eating reinforcement, and misleading nutrition claims.
- **A268 — Exercise-Safety Alchemy:** Reduce avoidable injury from unsuitable, excessive, or poorly adapted physical demands.
- **A269 — Sleep-Harm Prevention Alchemy:** Identify and reduce environmental, behavioral, and occupational factors that undermine safe and sufficient sleep.
- **A270 — Heat-and-Cold Safety Alchemy:** Prevent and respond appropriately to hazardous thermal exposure.
- **A271 — Reproductive-Autonomy Protection Alchemy:** Protect privacy, informed choice, bodily autonomy, and freedom from reproductive coercion.
- **A272 — Child- and Dependent-Body Safeguarding Alchemy:** Protect children and dependent people from abuse, neglect, exploitation, and non-consensual bodily harm.
- **A273 — Disability-Rights and Access Alchemy:** Prevent exclusion and ensure reasonable access, accommodation, and respect for individual agency.
- **A274 — Health-Information Privacy Alchemy:** Protect medical, genetic, biometric, reproductive, and other sensitive bodily information.
- **A275 — Anti-Body-Shaming Alchemy:** Prevent stigma, discrimination, harassment, and harmful treatment based on bodily appearance, size, disability, age, or variation.
- **A276 — Anti-Pseudoscience Alchemy:** Detect unsupported bodily claims, miracle-cure promises, fabricated evidence, and unsafe health misinformation.
- **A277 — Anti-Exploitation-in-Research Alchemy:** Prevent coercive, deceptive, or unfair human-subject research and require appropriate ethical oversight.
- **A278 — Clinical-Handoff Safety Alchemy:** Reduce loss, distortion, and omission of important information during transitions in care.
- **A279 — Adverse-Event Learning Alchemy:** Record, investigate, and learn from preventable harm without concealing errors or defaulting to blame.
- **A280 — Body-Data Security Alchemy:** Protect biological, genetic, imaging, wearable, and physiological data against unauthorized access, alteration, inference, or disclosure.

## Body Alchemy — Harm-pattern classifications for recognition and prevention only
These entries describe risks to recognize and prevent, not practices to perform.
- **BX001 — Bodily-Coercion Pattern:** Non-consensual bodily control or intervention. Defensive links: A148, A150, A261, A262.
- **BX002 — Medical-Neglect Pattern:** Failure to respond appropriately to legitimate care needs. Defensive links: A180, A193, A232, A240.
- **BX003 — Unsafe-Experimentation Pattern:** Research or intervention without adequate consent, safeguards, or oversight. Defensive links: A262, A277.
- **BX004 — Biological-Data-Exploitation Pattern:** Misuse of genetic, biometric, health, or physiological data. Defensive links: A250, A274, A280.
- **BX005 — Body-Image-Manipulation Pattern:** Exploitation of insecurity or stigma to drive harmful behavior or products. Defensive links: A267, A275, A276.
- **BX006 — Unsafe-Enhancement Pattern:** Unjustified or inadequately evaluated bodily alteration or performance enhancement. Defensive links: A200, A220, A230, A276.
- **BX007 — Discriminatory-Care Pattern:** Unequal or dismissive treatment based on identity, disability, age, appearance, or other irrelevant assumptions. Defensive links: A209, A273, A275.
- **BX008 — Health-Misinformation Pattern:** False or misleading claims about bodily function, diagnosis, prevention, or treatment. Defensive links: A199, A200, A276.
- **BX009 — Reproductive-Coercion Pattern:** Interference with reproductive autonomy or privacy. Defensive links: A187, A271, A262.
- **BX010 — Unsafe-Data-Inference Pattern:** Unsupported or unauthorized conclusions drawn from bodily measurements or biometrics. Defensive links: A192, A199, A250, A274.
- **BX011 — Unsafe-Workload Pattern:** Exposure to excessive physical demands or preventable workplace hazards. Defensive links: A230, A235, A240, A264.
- **BX012 — Recovery-Exploitation Pattern:** Pressure to resume activity or work before safe, individualized recovery. Defensive links: A198, A236, A248, A259.

## Body Alchemy record requirements
For each Body Alchemy entry, retain the universal record format and additionally specify:
- **Organism and population:** Human, other species, cell model, or population; do not generalize beyond the evidence.
- **Anatomical level:** Molecular, cellular, tissue, organ, organ-system, whole-body, or population.
- **Biological domain:** Anatomy, physiology, biochemistry, genetics, neuroscience, biomechanics, immunology, ecology, or another named field.
- **Normal variation and context:** Age, development, environment, disability, individual variation, and other relevant context.
- **Measurement limitations:** Method, uncertainty, reference population, bias, and limits of interpretation.
- **Health and safety boundary:** Whether the entry is descriptive, preventive, supportive, diagnostic-support, or intervention-related; do not turn taxonomy entries into unsupervised treatment instructions.
- **Consent and privacy:** Especially for bodily intervention, reproductive information, genetic data, biometrics, and research involving people.
- **Evidence and review:** Distinguish established evidence, emerging research, hypotheses, and metaphor.

## Initial cross-stage mapping
- **Aries:** bodily origin, identity, autonomy, development, and variation.
- **Taurus:** anatomy, cells, tissues, organs, and physical structures.
- **Gemini:** nervous systems, signals, sensation, movement control, and expression.
- **Cancer:** immunity, barriers, homeostasis, regulation, and protection.
- **Leo:** organ-system function, movement, metabolism, and expression.
- **Virgo:** measurement, observation, analysis, and error reduction.
- **Libra:** integration, balance, nutrition, accessibility, and individual needs.
- **Scorpio:** cellular change, repair, regeneration, adaptation, and dysregulation.
- **Sagittarius:** growth, learning, conditioning, and environmental adaptation.
- **Capricorn:** maintenance, prevention, ergonomics, and lifecycle stewardship.
- **Aquarius:** systems biology, biomechanics, bioinformatics, and body-technology interfaces.
- **Pisces:** restoration, rehabilitation, chronic support, palliative care, and dignity.

## Taxonomy maintenance
Body Alchemy entries are part of the shared Universal Taxonomy and must remain synchronized in C2O-OS, C2O-App, UNIVERSALIS-OS, and UNIVERSALIS-App. Implementation may differ by edition, but identifiers, definitions, evidence labels, and safety principles must not silently diverge. Future submissions may expand or split entries when evidence and conceptual boundaries justify doing so.


# Heart Alchemy — Domain Expansion
**Originating Alchemy:** Heart Alchemy  
**Family version:** 0.1.0  
**Parent family:** Body Alchemy  
**Zodiac stages:** All twelve.  
**Primary domain:** Cardiovascular anatomy, physiology, biology, biomechanics, clinical science, prevention, care, and restoration.  
**Related but distinct domains:** Emotional, psychological, cultural, and symbolic meanings of the heart. These may be cross-referenced but must never be mistaken for cardiac anatomy or physiology.

## Definition and boundaries
**Heart Alchemy** is the organized study and classification of the heart as an anatomical organ, a muscular pump, an electrical and mechanical system, a regulator within the cardiovascular network, a site of biological adaptation and disease, and a focus of prevention, care, recovery, and research.

The anatomical heart is not the whole cardiovascular system: blood vessels, blood, lungs, kidneys, nervous and endocrine regulation, and other systems interact with cardiac function. Entries must make those relationships explicit. Heart rate, blood pressure, cardiac output, rhythm, and other measurements are distinct variables and must not be used interchangeably.

The word "heart" also appears in emotional and symbolic language. Emotional experiences can have physiological correlates, but metaphorical claims (for example, "the heart knows") are not anatomical mechanisms. Psychological and social well-being matter, but must be classified with appropriate evidence and not reduced to cardiac physiology.

This taxonomy is educational and classificatory. It is not a diagnostic system, emergency protocol, or individualized treatment plan. Suspected heart attack, stroke, severe breathing difficulty, fainting with concerning symptoms, or other possible emergencies require immediate local emergency services; taxonomy content must never delay urgent care.

## Heart Alchemy list

### Zodiac I — Aries: Origin, identity, and emergence
- **A281 — Cardiac Identity Alchemy:** Define the heart's anatomical identity, boundaries, position, and relationships to surrounding structures.
- **A282 — Cardiogenesis Alchemy:** Study formation and development of the heart and major cardiac structures before birth.
- **A283 — Cardiac Lineage Alchemy:** Study the developmental origins and differentiation of cardiac cell populations.
- **A284 — Cardiac Morphogenesis Alchemy:** Study formation of chambers, septa, valves, outflow structures, and great-vessel connections.
- **A285 — Cardiac-Conduction Development Alchemy:** Study development of the specialized tissues responsible for initiating and conducting electrical impulses.
- **A286 — Congenital-Heart Variation Alchemy:** Classify congenital cardiac differences and their developmental context without treating all variation as equivalent.
- **A287 — Cardiac-Life-Course Alchemy:** Study how cardiac structure and function change from fetal development through infancy, adulthood, and later life.
- **A288 — Cardiac Autonomy and Consent Alchemy:** Protect bodily autonomy, informed consent, and privacy in cardiac testing, procedures, research, and monitoring.
- **A289 — Heart-Model Alchemy:** Build accurate, appropriately bounded anatomical and physiological representations of the heart.
- **A290 — Anti-Congenital-Stigma Alchemy:** Prevent discrimination, stigma, and assumptions about quality of life based on congenital cardiac differences.

### Zodiac II — Taurus: Structure, tissue, and material foundations
- **A291 — Cardiac-Anatomy Alchemy:** Study the heart's chambers, walls, surfaces, orientation, and anatomical relationships.
- **A292 — Myocardial Alchemy:** Study cardiac muscle structure, contraction, metabolism, and tissue properties.
- **A293 — Endocardial Alchemy:** Study the inner lining of the heart and its roles in chamber and valve interfaces.
- **A294 — Epicardial Alchemy:** Study the outer heart layer and its anatomical and biological relationships.
- **A295 — Pericardial Alchemy:** Study the pericardium, its layers, fluid space, and protective/supportive functions.
- **A296 — Cardiac-Valve Alchemy:** Study the mitral, tricuspid, aortic, and pulmonary valves, their structure, motion, and function.
- **A297 — Septal Alchemy:** Study the interatrial and interventricular septa and their roles in separating chambers.
- **A298 — Coronary-Vessel Alchemy:** Study coronary arteries, cardiac veins, and the vascular supply and drainage of heart tissue.
- **A299 — Cardiac-Matrix Alchemy:** Study extracellular matrix, connective tissue, and structural remodeling in the heart.
- **A300 — Anti-Structural-Damage Alchemy:** Prevent and identify avoidable structural injury, adverse remodeling, and damage to cardiac tissues and valves.

### Zodiac III — Gemini: Electrical signaling and cardiac communication
- **A301 — Cardiac-Electrophysiology Alchemy:** Study electrical properties and activity of cardiac cells and tissue.
- **A302 — Sinoatrial-Node Alchemy:** Study the primary natural pacemaker and its regulation.
- **A303 — Atrioventricular-Conduction Alchemy:** Study impulse transmission through the atrioventricular node and related conduction pathways.
- **A304 — His-Purkinje Alchemy:** Study specialized conduction structures that distribute electrical activation through the ventricles.
- **A305 — Cardiac-Rhythm Alchemy:** Classify normal rhythm, rhythm variation, and arrhythmias using established definitions.
- **A306 — Electrocardiographic Alchemy:** Study ECG acquisition, waveform interpretation, lead systems, limitations, and artifact.
- **A307 — Autonomic-Cardiac Regulation Alchemy:** Study sympathetic and parasympathetic influences on heart rate, conduction, and contractility.
- **A308 — Neurocardiac Interaction Alchemy:** Study bidirectional interactions between cardiac function and nervous-system regulation.
- **A309 — Cardiac-Mechanical Signaling Alchemy:** Study how electrical activation coordinates mechanical contraction and relaxation.
- **A310 — Anti-Arrhythmia-Misinterpretation Alchemy:** Reduce false interpretation, missed artifacts, unsupported self-diagnosis, and unsafe conclusions from rhythm data.

### Zodiac IV — Cancer: Circulation, protection, and regulation
- **A311 — Cardiac-Cycle Alchemy:** Study coordinated atrial and ventricular filling, contraction, ejection, and relaxation.
- **A312 — Hemodynamic Alchemy:** Study pressure, flow, resistance, preload, afterload, and their context-dependent relationships.
- **A313 — Cardiac-Output Alchemy:** Study the volume of blood pumped by the heart over time and the factors that influence it.
- **A314 — Coronary-Perfusion Alchemy:** Study blood flow supplying the myocardium and its regulation.
- **A315 — Oxygen-Delivery Alchemy:** Study interactions among cardiac output, blood oxygen content, circulation, and tissue oxygen delivery.
- **A316 — Blood-Pressure-Regulation Alchemy:** Study cardiac, vascular, renal, neural, and hormonal contributions to blood pressure.
- **A317 — Cardiac-Inflammation Alchemy:** Study inflammatory processes affecting cardiac tissue and their biological context.
- **A318 — Cardiac-Infection Alchemy:** Study infections involving cardiac structures, including endocardial, myocardial, and pericardial disease.
- **A319 — Cardiac-Stress-Response Alchemy:** Study cardiac responses to physical, physiological, and environmental stressors.
- **A320 — Anti-Cardiovascular-Risk Alchemy:** Reduce modifiable cardiovascular risks through evidence-based prevention while accounting for non-modifiable factors and social determinants.

### Zodiac V — Leo: Pump function and performance
- **A321 — Cardiac-Contractility Alchemy:** Study myocardial force generation and factors affecting contraction.
- **A322 — Ventricular-Function Alchemy:** Study right and left ventricular filling, contraction, ejection, and functional differences.
- **A323 — Atrial-Function Alchemy:** Study atrial contraction, filling, reservoir, and conduit functions.
- **A324 — Valve-Hemodynamics Alchemy:** Study flow across cardiac valves and the consequences of stenosis or regurgitation.
- **A325 — Cardiac-Energetics Alchemy:** Study energy production and use in cardiac cells, including oxygen and substrate requirements.
- **A326 — Exercise-Cardiology Alchemy:** Study cardiac responses and adaptations during physical activity and recovery.
- **A327 — Cardiorespiratory-Coupling Alchemy:** Study coordinated heart-lung function during rest, activity, and physiological challenge.
- **A328 — Cardiac-Reserve Alchemy:** Study the heart's capacity to adjust output to changing demands and the limits of that capacity.
- **A329 — Cardiac-Sound Alchemy:** Study heart sounds, murmurs, acoustic mechanisms, and the limitations of auscultation.
- **A330 — Anti-Performance-Pressure Alchemy:** Prevent unsafe exertion, coercive performance expectations, and unvalidated claims about optimizing cardiac performance.

### Zodiac VI — Virgo: Measurement, analysis, and verification
- **A331 — Echocardiographic Alchemy:** Study ultrasound assessment of cardiac structure, motion, valves, and hemodynamics, including method limitations.
- **A332 — Cardiac-Imaging Alchemy:** Classify cardiac imaging methods such as echocardiography, CT, MRI, and nuclear imaging by purpose and limitation.
- **A333 — Cardiac-Biomarker Alchemy:** Study biomarkers used in cardiac assessment, their clinical context, and limitations.
- **A334 — Exercise-Testing Alchemy:** Study supervised exercise-based assessment and its indications, risks, and interpretive limits.
- **A335 — Ambulatory-Monitoring Alchemy:** Study continuous or intermittent monitoring of rhythm, rate, and related measures.
- **A336 — Cardiac-Genetics Alchemy:** Study inherited contributors to cardiac conditions, genetic testing limits, counseling, and privacy.
- **A337 — Cardiac-Risk-Assessment Alchemy:** Study risk estimation and its population assumptions, uncertainty, and limitations.
- **A338 — Cardiac-Data-Quality Alchemy:** Validate provenance, completeness, calibration, timing, artifacts, and uncertainty in cardiac data.
- **A339 — Cardiac-Differential-Interpretation Alchemy:** Preserve multiple plausible explanations and appropriate clinical context when interpreting cardiac findings.
- **A340 — Anti-Overdiagnosis and Underdiagnosis Alchemy:** Reduce missed disease, false positives, inappropriate testing, premature closure, and overconfident interpretation.

### Zodiac VII — Libra: Integration and cardiovascular balance
- **A341 — Heart-Vessel Integration Alchemy:** Study the heart's relationship with arteries, veins, capillaries, and the systemic and pulmonary circulations.
- **A342 — Heart-Lung Integration Alchemy:** Study pulmonary circulation, gas exchange, and right-left heart interactions.
- **A343 — Heart-Kidney Integration Alchemy:** Study cardiorenal interactions involving perfusion, fluid balance, electrolytes, and regulatory signaling.
- **A344 — Heart-Brain Integration Alchemy:** Study relationships among cerebral perfusion, autonomic regulation, and cardiac function.
- **A345 — Heart-Endocrine Integration Alchemy:** Study hormonal and neurohormonal influences on cardiac and vascular regulation.
- **A346 — Heart-Metabolism Integration Alchemy:** Study relationships among cardiac function, glucose regulation, lipid metabolism, and energy balance.
- **A347 — Cardiac-Fluid-Balance Alchemy:** Study fluid distribution and its effects on cardiac filling and circulation.
- **A348 — Cardiac-Aging Integration Alchemy:** Study age-related cardiac and vascular changes while distinguishing typical variation from disease.
- **A349 — Cardiac-Equity Alchemy:** Study differences in cardiovascular outcomes, access, exposure, and care without attributing population disparities to unsupported biological stereotypes.
- **A350 — Anti-Systemic-Imbalance Alchemy:** Identify interacting contributors to cardiovascular dysfunction rather than treating one measurement as the entire system.

### Zodiac VIII — Scorpio: Disease, injury, and deep transformation
- **A351 — Ischemic-Heart Alchemy:** Study reduced myocardial blood supply and its mechanisms and consequences.
- **A352 — Myocardial-Infarction Alchemy:** Classify heart attack mechanisms, tissue injury, diagnostic evidence, and recovery research.
- **A353 — Heart-Failure Alchemy:** Study syndromes involving impaired cardiac filling or pumping and their diverse causes and presentations.
- **A354 — Cardiomyopathy Alchemy:** Study diseases of heart muscle, including structural, functional, genetic, and acquired forms.

- **A356 — Valvular-Heart-Disease Alchemy:** Study disorders affecting cardiac valve structure and function.
- **A357 — Pericardial-Disease Alchemy:** Study inflammatory, fluid-related, constrictive, and other pericardial disorders.
- **A358 — Cardiac-Remodeling Alchemy:** Study changes in heart structure and function following pressure, volume, ischemic, inflammatory, or other stresses.
- **A359 — Cardiac-Repair and Regeneration Research Alchemy:** Study repair, remodeling, and regenerative research while distinguishing experimental findings from established clinical capability.
- **A360 — Anti-Cardiac-Harm Alchemy:** Prevent avoidable progression and complications through evidence-based risk reduction, timely assessment, and appropriate care pathways.

### Zodiac IX — Sagittarius: Adaptation, learning, and capacity
- **A361 — Cardiac-Training Adaptation Alchemy:** Study cardiac and vascular adaptations to repeated physical activity.
- **A362 — Cardiac-Recovery Alchemy:** Study recovery after exertion, illness, injury, and cardiac procedures.
- **A363 — Cardiac-Rehabilitation Alchemy:** Classify structured, individualized rehabilitation and secondary-prevention approaches.
- **A364 — Cardiac-Development Alchemy:** Study changes in cardiac structure and function through growth and maturation.
- **A365 — Cardiac-Environmental Adaptation Alchemy:** Study responses to altitude, heat, cold, air quality, and other environmental exposures.

### Zodiac X — Capricorn: Maintenance, prevention, and stewardship

- **A372 — Blood-Pressure Stewardship Alchemy:** Study accurate measurement, longitudinal monitoring, and appropriate interpretation of blood pressure.
- **A373 — Lipid-Health Alchemy:** Study blood lipids, atherosclerotic risk, and evidence-based risk management.
- **A374 — Tobacco- and Nicotine-Risk Prevention Alchemy:** Study cardiovascular harms associated with tobacco and nicotine exposure and prevention strategies.
- **A375 — Diabetes-Cardiovascular Integration Alchemy:** Study relationships between diabetes, vascular health, and cardiac risk.
- **A376 — Nutrition-and-Heart Alchemy:** Study dietary patterns and cardiovascular evidence while avoiding universal or unsupported prescriptions.
- **A377 — Sleep-and-Heart Alchemy:** Study associations and mechanisms linking sleep, circadian regulation, and cardiovascular health.

- **A386 — Artificial-Intelligence Cardiology Alchemy:** Evaluate computational systems for cardiac tasks for validation, bias, explainability, oversight, and clinical limitations.

- **A388 — Cardiac-Research Collaboration Alchemy:** Coordinate reproducible, ethically governed research across disciplines and institutions.

### Zodiac XII — Pisces: Restoration, continuity, and dignity

- **A393 — Heart-Failure Support Alchemy:** Organize evidence-based long-term support, symptom awareness, and care continuity for people living with heart failure.
- **A394 — Cardiac-Symptom Quality-of-Life Alchemy:** Study symptom burden, daily function, participation, and individual goals.
- **A395 — Cardiac-Palliative Alchemy:** Study relief of suffering and quality-of-life support in advanced cardiovascular illness.
- **A396 — Cardiac-End-of-Life Dignity Alchemy:** Protect informed preferences, comfort, privacy, and dignity in serious or end-stage cardiac illness.

- **A398 — Cardiac-Device-Lifecycle Alchemy:** Study implantation, maintenance, monitoring, replacement, and end-of-life management of cardiac devices.
- **A399 — Cardiac-Knowledge Restoration Alchemy:** Preserve accurate records, patient knowledge, research findings, and lessons learned while correcting errors.
- **A400 — Anti-Recurrence and Continuity Alchemy:** Reduce avoidable recurrence and failures of follow-up without assigning blame to people affected by disease.

## Heart Alchemy — Cross-stage prevention, protection, and correction
- **A401 — Cardiac-Emergency Recognition Alchemy:** Improve recognition of potentially urgent cardiac warning signs and ensure that educational tools direct people to local emergency services rather than delaying care.
- **A402 — Cardiac-Misinterpretation Prevention Alchemy:** Prevent self-diagnosis and treatment decisions based solely on isolated heart-rate, ECG, wearable, or blood-pressure readings.
- **A403 — Cardiac-Medication Safety Alchemy:** Reduce medication errors, harmful interactions, and unsafe discontinuation through qualified review and reliable communication.
- **A404 — Cardiac-Procedure Consent Alchemy:** Protect informed consent, clear communication of benefits and risks, and respect for patient preferences.
- **A405 — Cardiac-Device Cybersecurity Alchemy:** Protect connected cardiac devices and associated systems against unauthorized access, unsafe modification, and data compromise.
- **A406 — Cardiac-Data Privacy Alchemy:** Protect ECGs, imaging, genetic results, device telemetry, and cardiovascular records.
- **A407 — Cardiac-Research Ethics Alchemy:** Ensure appropriate ethical review, participant protections, consent, transparency, and honest reporting.
- **A408 — Cardiac-Equity Protection Alchemy:** Identify and reduce barriers to prevention, diagnosis, treatment, rehabilitation, and follow-up.
- **A409 — Cardiac-Second-Review Alchemy:** Support appropriate independent review when findings are uncertain, high impact, or discordant.
- **A410 — Cardiac-Clinical-Handoff Alchemy:** Reduce errors during transfers among emergency, inpatient, outpatient, rehabilitation, and home settings.
- **A411 — Cardiac-Device Failure Prevention Alchemy:** Identify foreseeable device hazards, maintenance failures, alarms, and software or hardware faults.
- **A412 — Cardiac-Measurement Calibration Alchemy:** Validate measurement devices, acquisition conditions, calibration, and known sources of error.
- **A413 — Cardiac-Overtreatment Prevention Alchemy:** Reduce unnecessary interventions through evidence-based indications, shared decisions, and careful risk-benefit assessment.
- **A414 — Cardiac-Undertreatment Prevention Alchemy:** Reduce missed opportunities for appropriate assessment and evidence-based care.
- **A415 — Cardiac-Commercial-Conflict Protection Alchemy:** Disclose and manage conflicts of interest in devices, diagnostics, supplements, research, and treatment claims.
- **A416 — Cardiac-Misinformation Defense Alchemy:** Detect false cures, fabricated studies, miracle claims, and misleading interpretations of cardiac science.
- **A417 — Cardiac-Environmental Protection Alchemy:** Identify and reduce cardiovascular hazards associated with pollution, extreme heat, occupational exposure, and unsafe environments.
- **A418 — Cardiac-Rehabilitation Access Alchemy:** Reduce barriers to safe, individualized rehabilitation and sustained follow-up.
- **A419 — Cardiac-Incident Learning Alchemy:** Investigate adverse events and near misses, preserve evidence, and implement corrective action.
- **A420 — Cardiac-Restoration Verification Alchemy:** Verify that recovery claims, device performance, care processes, and outcome measures are supported by appropriate evidence.

## Heart Alchemy — Harm-pattern classifications for recognition and prevention only
These are risks to recognize and prevent, not instructions or constructive Alchemies.
- **BH001 — Cardiac-Misinformation Pattern:** False claims about heart function, diagnosis, prevention, or cures. Counter-Alchemies: A402, A416, A420.
- **BH002 — Emergency-Delay Pattern:** Advice or system behavior that delays urgent assessment. Counter-Alchemies: A380, A401.
- **BH003 — Wearable-Overclaim Pattern:** Treating consumer-device readings as definitive diagnoses. Counter-Alchemies: A338, A385, A402, A412.
- **BH004 — Cardiac-Data-Exploitation Pattern:** Unauthorized use of ECG, heart-rate, genetic, or device data. Counter-Alchemies: A390, A405, A406.
- **BH005 — Unsafe-Device-Modification Pattern:** Unauthorized or inadequately validated changes to cardiac devices or software. Counter-Alchemies: A383, A405, A411.
- **BH006 — Medication-Manipulation Pattern:** Encouraging unsafe starting, stopping, or changing of cardiac medication. Counter-Alchemies: A403, A416.
- **BH007 — Commercial-Cure-Deception Pattern:** Exploiting fear to sell unproven cardiac cures or products. Counter-Alchemies: A415, A416.
- **BH008 — Cardiac-Discrimination Pattern:** Unequal care or unsupported assumptions based on age, sex, disability, identity, or social status. Counter-Alchemies: A349, A408.
- **BH009 — Research-Coercion Pattern:** Exploiting people with cardiac conditions in research or data collection. Counter-Alchemies: A288, A404, A407.
- **BH010 — Device-Surveillance Pattern:** Coercive or hidden monitoring through connected cardiac technologies. Counter-Alchemies: A390, A405, A406.
- **BH011 — Unsafe-Performance-Enhancement Pattern:** Pressuring people to push cardiac limits or use unverified enhancement methods. Counter-Alchemies: A330, A370.
- **BH012 — Care-Continuity-Failure Pattern:** Lost results, medication discrepancies, uncommunicated risks, or missed follow-up. Counter-Alchemies: A379, A410, A419.

## Heart Alchemy record requirements
Each Heart Alchemy record must additionally identify:
- **Anatomical scope:** myocardium, endocardium, epicardium, pericardium, chambers, valves, septa, conduction system, coronary circulation, or connected cardiovascular structures.
- **Physiological scope:** electrical activity, contraction, relaxation, pressure, flow, perfusion, oxygen delivery, or regulation.
- **System interactions:** vascular, pulmonary, renal, neural, endocrine, metabolic, immune, and environmental relationships as applicable.
- **Population and context:** developmental stage, age, pregnancy where relevant, congenital variation, comorbidities, disability, and population assumptions.
- **Evidence type:** anatomical observation, physiological measurement, clinical study, guideline, systematic review, model, hypothesis, or metaphor.
- **Measurement limits:** acquisition method, calibration, artifact, uncertainty, and limits of inference.
- **Risk and urgency:** whether the subject is routine education, prevention, clinical assessment, or potentially time-critical; emergency warnings must not be buried.
- **Consent and privacy:** especially for genetic information, ECG, imaging, implanted devices, and remote monitoring.
- **Safety boundary:** no unsupervised medication, device, or treatment changes; clinical decisions require qualified care.

## Initial Heart Alchemy cross-stage map
- **Aries:** cardiac identity, formation, development, autonomy, and congenital variation.
- **Taurus:** heart anatomy, myocardium, valves, septa, pericardium, and coronary vessels.
- **Gemini:** conduction system, electrophysiology, rhythm, ECG, and neurocardiac signaling.
- **Cancer:** cardiac cycle, hemodynamics, perfusion, inflammation, infection, and protection.
- **Leo:** contraction, pumping, cardiac output, exercise response, and performance.
- **Virgo:** imaging, biomarkers, testing, data quality, risk interpretation, and diagnostic error.
- **Libra:** integration with vascular, pulmonary, renal, neural, endocrine, and metabolic systems.
- **Scorpio:** ischemia, infarction, heart failure, cardiomyopathy, arrhythmia, and remodeling.
- **Sagittarius:** adaptation, rehabilitation, recovery, environmental response, and learning.
- **Capricorn:** prevention, risk-factor stewardship, maintenance, and continuity of care.
- **Aquarius:** bioengineering, devices, monitoring, interoperability, AI, and population research.
- **Pisces:** restoration, transplantation, palliative support, dignity, and continuity.

## Taxonomy maintenance
Heart Alchemy is a specialized child family of Body Alchemy. Its entries must retain stable identifiers and cross-reference relevant Body Alchemy entries rather than duplicate them. Heart Alchemy content must be synchronized across C2O-OS, C2O-App, UNIVERSALIS-OS, and UNIVERSALIS-App. The four files must have identical taxonomy content and identifiers. Implementation and private governance details remain edition-specific; C2O private-source material must not be disclosed in the shared taxonomy.


# Mind Alchemy — Domain Expansion
**Originating Alchemy:** Mind Alchemy  
**Family version:** 0.1.0  
**Parent family:** Body Alchemy  
**Related family:** Heart Alchemy  
**Zodiac stages:** All twelve.  
**Primary domains:** Cognitive science, psychology, neuroscience, psychiatry, philosophy of mind, linguistics, learning sciences, human-computer interaction, and mental-health support.

## Definition, distinctions, and boundaries
**Mind Alchemy** is the organized study and classification of mental processes, capacities, experiences, representations, and changes, including perception, attention, memory, learning, reasoning, language, imagination, emotion, motivation, identity, self-regulation, decision-making, and conscious experience.

Mind is embodied and influenced by brain, body, relationships, culture, development, and environment. The mind is not identical to the brain: brain structures and neural activity are biological subjects, while mental processes and subjective experience require appropriate psychological, cognitive, behavioral, and philosophical descriptions. Mind Alchemy cross-references Body Alchemy and Heart Alchemy without collapsing these distinctions.

Mental health is not simply the absence of distress, and mental illness is not a moral failing or a person's identity. Mental experiences and abilities vary across individuals and contexts. Neurodivergence and disability must not be automatically classified as defects. Diagnostic labels require appropriate professional assessment; this taxonomy is not a diagnostic or treatment system.

Consciousness, free will, the nature of subjective experience, and some theories of cognition remain active scientific and philosophical questions. Entries about these subjects must label competing theories and uncertainty. Metaphors, spiritual interpretations, and philosophical positions must not be presented as settled empirical mechanisms.

The taxonomy is educational and classificatory, not individualized mental-health care. It must not replace professional support or emergency help. If someone may be in immediate danger or unable to stay safe, they should contact local emergency services or an appropriate crisis service; taxonomy content must never delay urgent support.

## Mind Alchemy list

### Zodiac I — Aries: Emergence, awareness, and mental identity
- **A421 — Mind Identity Alchemy:** Describe mental identity and experience without reducing a person to a diagnosis, test score, or single trait.
- **A422 — Cognitive Emergence Alchemy:** Study the development and emergence of cognitive capacities across individuals and the lifespan.
- **A423 — Awareness Alchemy:** Study awareness of external events, internal states, and ongoing mental activity.
- **A424 — Attention Initiation Alchemy:** Study how attention is oriented, initiated, and directed toward information or tasks.
- **A425 — Self-Awareness Alchemy:** Study recognition of one's thoughts, feelings, actions, and characteristics.
- **A426 — Metacognitive Emergence Alchemy:** Study awareness and regulation of one's own thinking and learning.
- **A427 — Mental Representation Alchemy:** Study how people represent objects, events, concepts, relationships, and possibilities.
- **A428 — Cognitive Development Alchemy:** Study changes in cognition from early development through adulthood and aging.
- **A429 — Individual Cognitive Variation Alchemy:** Describe differences in cognitive styles, capacities, and experiences without unsupported ranking or stigma.
- **A430 — Anti-Mental-Coercion Alchemy:** Prevent coercive control, forced belief, intimidation, and violations of psychological autonomy.

### Zodiac II — Taurus: Foundations, brain, and mental structures
- **A431 — Brain-Mind Relationship Alchemy:** Study relationships between brain processes, cognition, behavior, and subjective experience while preserving conceptual distinctions.
- **A432 — Neural-Network Alchemy:** Study neural circuits and networks relevant to mental processes.
- **A433 — Cognitive-Architecture Alchemy:** Model interacting cognitive functions and their dependencies.
- **A434 — Perceptual-Foundation Alchemy:** Study mechanisms and processes through which sensory information is organized into perception.
- **A435 — Memory-Architecture Alchemy:** Study different memory systems, their functions, and interactions.
- **A436 — Language-Architecture Alchemy:** Study mental and neural processes involved in language comprehension, production, and representation.
- **A437 — Concept-Formation Alchemy:** Study how concepts and categories are acquired, represented, and revised.
- **A438 — Belief-Structure Alchemy:** Study how beliefs are formed, organized, maintained, and updated.
- **A439 — Cognitive-Resource Alchemy:** Study limits and distribution of working memory, attention, and other cognitive resources.
- **A440 — Anti-Cognitive-Reduction Alchemy:** Prevent the false reduction of complex mental phenomena to one brain region, biomarker, test, or simplistic model.

### Zodiac III — Gemini: Communication, perception, and connection
- **A441 — Sensory-Perception Alchemy:** Study interpretation of visual, auditory, tactile, olfactory, gustatory, vestibular, and other sensory information.
- **A442 — Selective-Attention Alchemy:** Study selection of relevant information amid competing stimuli.
- **A443 — Sustained-Attention Alchemy:** Study maintaining attention over time and across changing task demands.
- **A444 — Divided-Attention Alchemy:** Study management of multiple information streams and task demands, including its limits.
- **A445 — Language-Comprehension Alchemy:** Study interpretation of spoken, written, signed, and other linguistic communication.
- **A446 — Language-Expression Alchemy:** Study formulation and expression of thoughts through language and communication systems.
- **A447 — Social-Cognition Alchemy:** Study perception and interpretation of other people's actions, intentions, emotions, and social contexts.
- **A448 — Perspective-Taking Alchemy:** Study understanding that other people may hold different information, beliefs, feelings, and viewpoints.
- **A449 — Mental-Communication Alchemy:** Study communication of internal experiences and mental states while recognizing that no person can directly read another's mind.
- **A450 — Anti-Communication-Manipulation Alchemy:** Detect and prevent deceptive framing, coercive persuasion, targeted confusion, and exploitative information practices.

- **A451 — Emotion Alchemy:** Study emotional experiences, functions, expression, and regulation.
- **A452 — Emotional-Awareness Alchemy:** Study identifying, differentiating, and describing emotional states.
- **A453 — Emotion-Regulation Alchemy:** Study strategies and processes for modulating emotional responses without requiring suppression or invalidation.
