## Cross-domain safeguards for both editions
- **Autonomy and consent:** No domain profile permits coercion, non-consensual experimentation, hidden profiling, or overriding a person's lawful choices.
- **Privacy and data minimization:** Collect only necessary data, state purpose and retention, restrict access, and provide appropriate correction and deletion pathways.
- **Evidence integrity:** Preserve source, method, population/context, uncertainty, limitations, and independent review. A taxonomy entry is not evidence that a capability is implemented or effective.
- **Human accountability:** Consequential actions require an identified accountable authority, meaningful review, appeal where appropriate, and a safe override.
- **Cultural and belief pluralism:** Soul and Spirit material must not impose, rank, or erase religious, spiritual, secular, Indigenous, atheist, or agnostic perspectives; community-restricted knowledge requires permission.
- **Consciousness uncertainty:** Do not infer definitive consciousness, sentience, personhood, or absence thereof from a single behavior, benchmark, model, or theory.
- **Safety and escalation:** Medical, psychological, crisis, and other high-risk situations require appropriate qualified or emergency support; taxonomy content must never delay urgent help.
- **Edition separation:** Shared contracts may define interoperable formats and public threat classifications, but must not transfer C2O secrets, confidential policies, or private incident records into UNIVERSALIS.

## Application Alchemy — Shared Application Architecture and Edition Profiles
**Originating Alchemy:** Application Alchemy  
**Family version:** 0.1.0  
**Architecture:** One underlying application system with two editions: C2O private-source/company and UNIVERSALIS public/open-source.  
**Boundary:** Applications consume OS services through documented, least-privilege interfaces. They do not implement or assume kernel authority, bypass OS security boundaries, or silently inherit administrative privileges.
### Definition and scope
Application Alchemy classifies the design, creation, packaging, execution, interaction, integration, governance, maintenance, and retirement of software applications and their supporting application services. It includes native, web, mobile, desktop, command-line, distributed, assistive, and agent-integrated applications. It does not collapse application responsibilities into operating-system responsibilities, nor imply that every application requires every platform or execution model.
### Zodiac I — Aries: Initiation and application identity

### Zodiac I — Aries: initiation and emergence
- **A1121 — Application Identity Alchemy:** Identify an application's purpose, ownership, supported users, and bounded responsibilities.
- **A1122 — Application Purpose Alchemy:** Translate intended outcomes into explicit, testable application requirements.
- **A1123 — Requirements Elicitation Alchemy:** Elicit requirements from affected users and stakeholders with accessible participation.
- **A1124 — User and Stakeholder Discovery Alchemy:** Map user contexts and constraints without assuming a single typical user.
- **A1125 — Use Case Definition Alchemy:** Define use cases, actors, preconditions, outcomes, and failure paths.
- **A1126 — Scope and Boundary Alchemy:** Specify application boundaries, exclusions, and dependencies.
- **A1127 — Application Consent Alchemy:** Obtain meaningful consent for optional data use and consequential interactions.
- **A1128 — Authorization Model Alchemy:** Integrate explicit identity and authorization requirements from the OS.
- **A1129 — Feasibility Assessment Alchemy:** Assess technical, operational, legal, accessibility, and safety feasibility.
- **A1130 — Safe Initiation Alchemy:** Initialize safely with secure defaults and a reversible first-run path.

### Zodiac II — Taurus: foundations and source integrity
- **A1131 — Application Architecture Alchemy:** Define application structure, components, and responsibilities.
- **A1132 — Component Modeling Alchemy:** Model component boundaries and their contracts.
- **A1133 — Data Modeling Alchemy:** Model data entities, relationships, classification, and lifecycle.
- **A1134 — State Management Alchemy:** Manage application state with explicit invariants and transitions.
- **A1135 — Persistence Design Alchemy:** Design persistence with integrity, retention, and recovery controls.
- **A1136 — Resource Budgeting Alchemy:** Budget CPU, memory, storage, network, and energy resources.
- **A1137 — Dependency Selection Alchemy:** Select dependencies with provenance, maintenance, and license review.
- **A1138 — Platform Compatibility Alchemy:** Define supported platforms and compatibility limits.
- **A1139 — Accessibility Foundations Alchemy:** Build accessibility into foundational components and workflows.
- **A1140 — Integrity Baseline Alchemy:** Establish integrity baselines for source, packages, and runtime artifacts.

### Zodiac III — Gemini: connection and communication
- **A1141 — API Contract Alchemy:** Specify stable, versioned application interfaces.
- **A1142 — Interface Composition Alchemy:** Compose interface elements with consistent semantics and accessibility.
- **A1143 — Inter Application Communication Alchemy:** Define authenticated and authorized inter-application communication.
- **A1144 — Event and Message Flow Alchemy:** Govern events, queues, and message delivery semantics.
- **A1145 — Authentication Integration Alchemy:** Integrate identity without leaking credentials or over-scoping access.
- **A1146 — Authorization Integration Alchemy:** Enforce least-privilege authorization for application actions.
- **A1147 — Data Exchange Alchemy:** Exchange data using documented schemas and purpose limitations.
- **A1148 — Protocol Translation Alchemy:** Translate protocols while preserving semantics and validation.
- **A1149 — Collaboration Interfaces Alchemy:** Support collaboration with clear roles, attribution, and consent.
- **A1150 — Communication Error Detection Alchemy:** Detect ambiguity, loss, duplication, and malformed communication.

### Zodiac IV — Cancer: care and protection
- **A1151 — User Privacy Alchemy:** Minimize personal data and make its use understandable.
- **A1152 — Data Protection Alchemy:** Protect application data in transit, at rest, and during processing.
- **A1153 — Session Safety Alchemy:** Protect sessions against theft, fixation, and unintended persistence.
- **A1154 — Account Recovery Alchemy:** Provide secure, accessible account recovery without weakening identity checks.
- **A1155 — User Safeguarding Alchemy:** Protect users from exploitation, harassment, and unsafe defaults.
- **A1156 — Consent Management Alchemy:** Record and honor granular, revocable consent.
- **A1157 — Backup and Sync Alchemy:** Provide recoverable backups and transparent synchronization.
- **A1158 — Accessibility Continuity Alchemy:** Preserve accessibility settings and assistive workflows across failures.
- **A1159 — Failure Safe Interaction Alchemy:** Fail safely without exposing data or trapping users.
- **A1160 — Abuse Prevention Alchemy:** Prevent abusive workflows, coercive patterns, and unauthorized use.

### Zodiac V — Leo: expression and manifestation
- **A1161 — User Experience Alchemy:** Design understandable, coherent user experiences.
- **A1162 — Interaction Design Alchemy:** Create usable interactions aligned with user goals and context.
- **A1163 — Workflow Composition Alchemy:** Compose workflows with clear states, reversibility, and error handling.
- **A1164 — Content Presentation Alchemy:** Present information with provenance and appropriate uncertainty.
- **A1165 — Notification Design Alchemy:** Make notifications relevant, controllable, and non-manipulative.
- **A1166 — Personalization Alchemy:** Personalize without discriminatory inference or loss of user control.
- **A1167 — Application Automation Alchemy:** Automate bounded tasks with visibility, logs, and override.
- **A1168 — Multimedia Expression Alchemy:** Support accessible media handling and user-controlled expression.
- **A1169 — Extension Mechanisms Alchemy:** Govern extensions through permissions, isolation, and review.
- **A1170 — Attribution and Licensing Alchemy:** Preserve authorship, licenses, and contribution provenance.

### Zodiac VI — Virgo: analysis and verification
- **A1171 — Functional Testing Alchemy:** Test functional behavior against explicit requirements.
- **A1172 — Usability Testing Alchemy:** Evaluate usability with representative and consented participation.
- **A1173 — Accessibility Testing Alchemy:** Test accessibility against stated standards and real assistive workflows.
- **A1174 — Performance Measurement Alchemy:** Measure performance with reproducible workloads and limits.
- **A1175 — Reliability Testing Alchemy:** Test reliability, resilience, and degraded operation.
- **A1176 — Security Testing Alchemy:** Assess security through authorized, scoped testing.
- **A1177 — Privacy Assessment Alchemy:** Assess privacy flows, defaults, retention, and data access.
- **A1178 — Compatibility Verification Alchemy:** Verify compatibility across supported environments.
- **A1179 — Regression Testing Alchemy:** Detect regressions across releases and configuration changes.
- **A1180 — Defect Correction Alchemy:** Correct defects with traceable fixes and regression coverage.

### Zodiac VII — Libra: balance and governance
- **A1181 — Application Governance Alchemy:** Define application decision rights and accountable maintainers.
- **A1182 — Role and Responsibility Alchemy:** Separate development, review, release, and operations responsibilities.
- **A1183 — Permission Transparency Alchemy:** Explain permissions and consequential application behavior.
- **A1184 — Data Sovereignty Alchemy:** Preserve user control over data location, export, and deletion.
- **A1185 — Fair Access Alchemy:** Evaluate equitable access and disparate impact.
- **A1186 — Ethical Design Alchemy:** Apply explicit ethical requirements to design and deployment.
- **A1187 — Decision Traceability Alchemy:** Trace important decisions and state changes without excess data capture.
- **A1188 — Conflict Resolution Alchemy:** Resolve disputes through documented, reviewable procedures.
- **A1189 — Auditability Alchemy:** Support independent audit and reproducible findings.
- **A1190 — Anti Corruption Alchemy:** Prevent conflicts of interest and concealed governance capture.

### Zodiac VIII — Scorpio: transformation and resistance
- **A1191 — Threat Modeling Alchemy:** Model assets, trust boundaries, threats, and abuse cases.
- **A1192 — Input Validation Alchemy:** Validate untrusted input and encode output safely.
- **A1193 — Sandboxing Alchemy:** Isolate application components and extensions by least privilege.
- **A1194 — Secure Dependency Handling Alchemy:** Verify dependency provenance and protect update paths.
- **A1195 — Secret Management Alchemy:** Store and rotate secrets using approved OS facilities.
- **A1196 — Abuse Resistance Alchemy:** Resist fraud, spam, impersonation, and abusive automation.
- **A1197 — Isolation and Containment Alchemy:** Contain compromise and limit blast radius.
- **A1198 — Incident Response Alchemy:** Respond to incidents with evidence preservation and user protection.
- **A1199 — Tamper Detection Alchemy:** Detect unauthorized modification of code, data, and runtime state.
- **A1200 — Destructive Action Prevention Alchemy:** Prevent destructive operations through authorization, preview, and recovery.

### Zodiac IX — Sagittarius: learning and expansion
- **A1201 — Developer Experience Alchemy:** Provide maintainable tools and workflows for developers.
- **A1202 — Documentation Alchemy:** Document behavior, contracts, setup, and limitations.
- **A1203 — Localization Alchemy:** Localize language and formats without breaking meaning.
- **A1204 — Portability Alchemy:** Support migration across platforms and environments.
- **A1205 — Interoperability Alchemy:** Interoperate through open, versioned contracts.
- **A1206 — Extensibility Alchemy:** Extend capabilities without hidden privileged coupling.
- **A1207 — Learning and Onboarding Alchemy:** Enable learning and onboarding with accurate guidance.
- **A1208 — Migration Planning Alchemy:** Plan safe data and configuration migration.
- **A1209 — Standards Alignment Alchemy:** Align with relevant standards and accessible conventions.
- **A1210 — Scope Control Alchemy:** Prevent scope creep and unsupported generalization.

### Zodiac X — Capricorn: operations and accountability
- **A1211 — Release Management Alchemy:** Release validated builds with provenance and rollback.
- **A1212 — Versioning Alchemy:** Version APIs, data formats, and application behavior.
- **A1213 — Update Management Alchemy:** Deliver authenticated, staged, recoverable updates.
- **A1214 — Configuration Management Alchemy:** Manage configuration securely and transparently.
- **A1215 — Operational Monitoring Alchemy:** Monitor health with privacy-preserving observability.
- **A1216 — Cost and Energy Optimization Alchemy:** Optimize cost and energy without compromising correctness or safety.
- **A1217 — Support Lifecycle Alchemy:** Define support, maintenance, and end-of-life obligations.
- **A1218 — Change Control Alchemy:** Review and authorize consequential changes.
- **A1219 — Service Level Management Alchemy:** Define service expectations and communicate limitations.
- **A1220 — Accountable Retirement Alchemy:** Retire applications safely with export, deletion, and transition support.

### Zodiac XI — Aquarius: systems and collective coordination
- **A1221 — Distributed Application Coordination Alchemy:** Coordinate distributed components with explicit trust and failure models.
- **A1222 — Local First Operation Alchemy:** Support local-first operation where appropriate.
- **A1223 — Offline Resilience Alchemy:** Remain useful under network loss with clear sync boundaries.
- **A1224 — Synchronization Alchemy:** Resolve synchronization conflicts transparently and safely.
- **A1225 — Federation Alchemy:** Federate across independently governed services.
- **A1226 — Plugin Ecosystem Governance Alchemy:** Review plugins and extensions under transparent rules.
- **A1227 — UNIVERSALINS Integration Alchemy:** Integrate UNIVERSALINS as non-personal assistants with scoped permissions.
- **A1228 — Guardian Integration Alchemy:** Integrate UNIVERSALIN Guardians as personal assistants under human control.
- **A1229 — Human Control of Automation Alchemy:** Keep automated actions attributable, reviewable, and overridable.
- **A1230 — Collective Intelligence Interfaces Alchemy:** Support collective work without erasing individual rights or attribution.

### Zodiac XII — Pisces: restoration and renewal
- **A1231 — Data and State Restoration Alchemy:** Restore verified application data and state.
- **A1232 — Rollback and Recovery Alchemy:** Rollback failed releases without silently losing user work.
- **A1233 — Application Reinstallation Alchemy:** Reinstall from authenticated sources and recover user configuration.
- **A1234 — Secure Reset Alchemy:** Reset securely while preserving lawful user export choices.
- **A1235 — Migration Recovery Alchemy:** Recover from migration failure with validated rollback paths.
- **A1236 — Incident Forensics Alchemy:** Conduct authorized forensics with privacy and retention limits.
- **A1237 — Data Export and Portability Alchemy:** Enable usable data export in documented formats.
- **A1238 — Account Closure Alchemy:** Close accounts and revoke credentials securely.
- **A1239 — End of Life Transition Alchemy:** Transition end-of-life users and data with clear notice and options.
- **A1240 — Continuous Improvement Alchemy:** Feed verified lessons into controlled, versioned improvement.

### Application Alchemy — cross-stage prevention, protection, detection, correction, recovery, and restoration
- **A1241 — Application-Access-Control Alchemy:** Prevent unauthorized application access through secure defaults, explicit identity, and least privilege.
- **A1242 — Application-Privacy-Protection Alchemy:** Protect personal and confidential data through minimization, encryption, purpose limitation, and retention controls.
- **A1243 — Application-Supply-Chain-Defense Alchemy:** Detect malicious or compromised dependencies through provenance, vulnerability monitoring, and integrity verification.
- **A1244 — Anti-Deceptive-Interface Alchemy:** Prevent deceptive or coercive interfaces through user testing, accessibility review, and transparent interaction rules.
- **A1245 — Application-Compromise-Containment Alchemy:** Contain compromised components through sandboxing, scoped tokens, isolation, and revocation.
- **A1246 — Human-Controlled-Automation Alchemy:** Prevent unsafe automation through bounded permissions, human confirmation, action previews, and override.
- **A1247 — Application-Privacy-Monitoring Alchemy:** Detect privacy violations and unexpected data flows through proportionate, privacy-preserving observability.
- **A1248 — Application-Defect-Correction Alchemy:** Correct defects through traceable remediation, regression testing, and disclosed limitations.
- **A1249 — Application-Recovery-Verification Alchemy:** Recover application data and service through tested backups, rollback, export, and restoration verification.
- **A1250 — Public-Contribution-Protection Alchemy:** Protect public contributions through review, signed provenance, reproducible builds, and maintainership accountability.
- **A1251 — Private-Source-Confidentiality Alchemy:** Protect C2O confidential code, credentials, policies, and incident records through compartmentalization and release scrutiny.
- **A1252 — Edition-Independence-Verification Alchemy:** Verify that UNIVERSALIS remains independently buildable and operable without C2O private services or authorization.
- **A1253 — Accessibility-Regression-Protection Alchemy:** Prevent accessibility regressions and preserve assistive technology compatibility across updates.
- **A1254 — Migration-Integrity-Protection Alchemy:** Prevent unsafe data migration, schema corruption, and irreversible loss through staged migration and rollback.
- **A1255 — Application-End-of-Life-Protection Alchemy:** Ensure end-of-life processes include notice, data portability, credential revocation, and secure deletion.

### Application Alchemy — harmful-pattern classifications for recognition and prevention only
- **AX001 — Privilege-boundary bypass Pattern:** An application or extension obtains authority beyond its approved scope. Defensive purpose only; apply prevention, detection, containment, correction, recovery, and accountable review.
- **AX002 — Covert data collection Pattern:** Personal data is collected, inferred, or transmitted without clear authorization or purpose. Defensive purpose only; apply prevention, detection, containment, correction, recovery, and accountable review.
- **AX003 — Supply-chain compromise Pattern:** A dependency, build, package, or update introduces malicious or untrusted behavior. Defensive purpose only; apply prevention, detection, containment, correction, recovery, and accountable review.
- **AX004 — Dark-pattern coercion Pattern:** Interface design manipulates users into choices they did not freely intend. Defensive purpose only; apply prevention, detection, containment, correction, recovery, and accountable review.
- **AX005 — Unsafe automation Pattern:** Automated actions cause consequential effects without adequate scope, review, or override. Defensive purpose only; apply prevention, detection, containment, correction, recovery, and accountable review.
- **AX006 — Unrecoverable data change Pattern:** A release or migration destroys or corrupts user data without tested recovery. Defensive purpose only; apply prevention, detection, containment, correction, recovery, and accountable review.
- **AX007 — Hidden C2O dependency Pattern:** UNIVERSALIS application functionality silently depends on private C2O infrastructure. Defensive purpose only; apply prevention, detection, containment, correction, recovery, and accountable review.
- **AX008 — Private-material leakage Pattern:** C2O source, secrets, policies, or confidential records enter public artifacts. Defensive purpose only; apply prevention, detection, containment, correction, recovery, and accountable review.
- **AX009 — Governance capture Pattern:** A maintainer or vendor gains opaque, unaccountable control over application direction. Defensive purpose only; apply prevention, detection, containment, correction, recovery, and accountable review.
- **AX010 — False verification claim Pattern:** Tests or audits are represented as proof beyond their actual coverage. Defensive purpose only; apply prevention, detection, containment, correction, recovery, and accountable review.
- **AX011 — Assistant authority confusion Pattern:** UNIVERSALINS or Guardians are granted implicit administrative or decision authority. Defensive purpose only; apply prevention, detection, containment, correction, recovery, and accountable review.
- **AX012 — Abandonment without transition Pattern:** Users lose support, data access, or security updates without a safe migration path. Defensive purpose only; apply prevention, detection, containment, correction, recovery, and accountable review.

### C2O Application Alchemy — private-source/company edition profile
C2O uses the shared application architecture with private-source stewardship, confidential operational controls, and company release governance. These controls do not apply to UNIVERSALIS by default and do not authorize disclosure.
- **C2OAPP001 — C2O Aries Application Stewardship Alchemy:** Govern Private-source custody and repository access under documented company authority, least privilege, traceable review, and confidential handling.
- **C2OAPP002 — C2O Taurus Application Stewardship Alchemy:** Govern Confidential product requirements and roadmaps under documented company authority, least privilege, traceable review, and confidential handling.
- **C2OAPP003 — C2O Gemini Application Stewardship Alchemy:** Govern Internal identity and release authorization under documented company authority, least privilege, traceable review, and confidential handling.
- **C2OAPP004 — C2O Cancer Application Stewardship Alchemy:** Govern Private signing-key custody and rotation under documented company authority, least privilege, traceable review, and confidential handling.
- **C2OAPP005 — C2O Leo Application Stewardship Alchemy:** Govern Confidential telemetry and incident stewardship under documented company authority, least privilege, traceable review, and confidential handling.
- **C2OAPP006 — C2O Virgo Application Stewardship Alchemy:** Govern Proprietary dependency and license governance under documented company authority, least privilege, traceable review, and confidential handling.
- **C2OAPP007 — C2O Libra Application Stewardship Alchemy:** Govern Internal build, test, and deployment segmentation under documented company authority, least privilege, traceable review, and confidential handling.
- **C2OAPP008 — C2O Scorpio Application Stewardship Alchemy:** Govern Company change approval and rollback under documented company authority, least privilege, traceable review, and confidential handling.
- **C2OAPP009 — C2O Sagittarius Application Stewardship Alchemy:** Govern Private customer and operational data protection under documented company authority, least privilege, traceable review, and confidential handling.
- **C2OAPP010 — C2O Capricorn Application Stewardship Alchemy:** Govern Authorized disclosure and public-release review under documented company authority, least privilege, traceable review, and confidential handling.
- **C2OAPP011 — C2O Aquarius Application Stewardship Alchemy:** Govern Internal support continuity and service accountability under documented company authority, least privilege, traceable review, and confidential handling.
- **C2OAPP012 — C2O Pisces Application Stewardship Alchemy:** Govern Private application retirement and records disposition under documented company authority, least privilege, traceable review, and confidential handling.

### UNIVERSALIS Application Alchemy — public/open-source edition profile
UNIVERSALIS uses the same underlying application architecture but remains publicly inspectable and independently buildable, operable, forkable, and maintainable under its selected open-source licenses and public governance.
- **UNIAPP001 — UNIVERSALIS Aries Public Application Alchemy:** Provide Public source and application-contract transparency without requiring C2O private services, credentials, approval, or unpublished components.
- **UNIAPP002 — UNIVERSALIS Taurus Public Application Alchemy:** Provide Independent build, installation, and operation without requiring C2O private services, credentials, approval, or unpublished components.
- **UNIAPP003 — UNIVERSALIS Gemini Public Application Alchemy:** Provide Open roadmap and accountable public governance without requiring C2O private services, credentials, approval, or unpublished components.
- **UNIAPP004 — UNIVERSALIS Cancer Public Application Alchemy:** Provide Transparent contribution and maintainer review without requiring C2O private services, credentials, approval, or unpublished components.
- **UNIAPP005 — UNIVERSALIS Leo Public Application Alchemy:** Provide Reproducible public application builds without requiring C2O private services, credentials, approval, or unpublished components.
- **UNIAPP006 — UNIVERSALIS Virgo Public Application Alchemy:** Provide Public release provenance and security advisories without requiring C2O private services, credentials, approval, or unpublished components.
- **UNIAPP007 — UNIVERSALIS Libra Public Application Alchemy:** Provide Open standards and user data portability without requiring C2O private services, credentials, approval, or unpublished components.
- **UNIAPP008 — UNIVERSALIS Scorpio Public Application Alchemy:** Provide Community continuity and forkability without requiring C2O private services, credentials, approval, or unpublished components.
- **UNIAPP009 — UNIVERSALIS Sagittarius Public Application Alchemy:** Provide Transparent privacy, telemetry, and retention without requiring C2O private services, credentials, approval, or unpublished components.
- **UNIAPP010 — UNIVERSALIS Capricorn Public Application Alchemy:** Provide Accessible documentation and contribution paths without requiring C2O private services, credentials, approval, or unpublished components.
- **UNIAPP011 — UNIVERSALIS Aquarius Public Application Alchemy:** Provide Independent security assessment and remediation without requiring C2O private services, credentials, approval, or unpublished components.
- **UNIAPP012 — UNIVERSALIS Pisces Public Application Alchemy:** Provide Public end-of-life and migration continuity without requiring C2O private services, credentials, approval, or unpublished components.

### Application edition-specific harm patterns — recognition and prevention only
- **C2OAPPX01 — Confidential application material exposure:** Private source, keys, internal policy, customer data, or incident records are disclosed without authorization.
- **C2OAPPX02 — Unaccountable private release:** A company application change bypasses authorized review, provenance, testing, or rollback.
- **C2OAPPX03 — Private authority leakage:** C2O-only controls or credentials are embedded in shared/public application contracts.
- **UNIAPPX01 — False open-source independence:** A public application cannot be built or operated without undisclosed private dependencies.
- **UNIAPPX02 — Opaque public governance:** Contribution, release, or security decisions are made without the declared public process.
- **UNIAPPX03 — Public artifact integrity failure:** Public packages lack trustworthy provenance, reproducible verification, or a responsible update path.

## Application Alchemy required record fields
Every application entry or implementation trace must identify: application purpose and user context; lifecycle stage; supported platform and execution model; data classes and processing purpose; inputs, outputs, and state transitions; OS interfaces and permission scope; dependencies and provenance; accessibility and localization requirements; threat model and privacy controls; failure, rollback, and recovery behavior; edition boundary; evidence status; verification method and limitations; accountable owner; and change/release authorization.

## Application Alchemy mandatory lifecycle
Every constructive application capability must identify preventive, protective, detective, containment, corrective, recovery, and restoration controls. Successful nominal operation is insufficient without safe failure, user control, verified recovery, and accountable maintenance.

## Relationship to the six domain Alchemies
Applications may support Body, Heart, Mind, Soul, Spirit, and Consciousness domains, but must not claim domain authority merely because they provide an interface or data-processing function. Health-related applications must not be treated as diagnosis or treatment systems without appropriate evidence, regulation, and qualified oversight. Soul and Spirit applications must preserve freedom of belief and cultural/community permissions. Mind and Consciousness applications must not claim to read minds or establish sentience from unsupported inference. All applications must respect bodily autonomy, privacy, accessibility, dignity, and consent.

## Taxonomy maintenance for v0.9.0
This release preserves the existing taxonomy and application expansion. See the appended v0.10.0 Trait Manipulation expansion for the next additive family.


# Trait Manipulation Alchemy — v0.10.0 expansion

## Source interpretation and scope
The supplied Superpower Wiki page describes Trait Manipulation as a fictional power to create, shape, alter, insert, or remove traits, including personality, genetic, and mental traits. It associates the concept with Attribute, Aspect, and Status Manipulation and lists limitations such as trait-specific scope and target immunity. This is a fictional capability taxonomy, not evidence that such supernatural manipulation is possible or scientifically validated. The source's own style guidance separately notes that its “Trait” namespace generally describes modifiers of powers rather than ordinary traits of people; this taxonomy therefore distinguishes (a) traits of people and living organisms, (b) traits of systems and applications, and (c) modifiers of powers or capabilities in fictional models. Do not collapse these categories.

**Operational definition:** Trait Manipulation Alchemy is the bounded study, description, voluntary development, configuration, protection, or authorized modification of a characteristic of a person, organism, artifact, model, or system. It does not authorize non-consensual human modification, identity control, genetic intervention, psychological reprogramming, or discriminatory profiling. For people, self-determination, bodily integrity, privacy, consent, and rights take precedence. For systems, changes require authorized ownership, testing, provenance, and rollback. Fictional/symbolic entries must be labeled as such.

## Core Trait Manipulation Alchemies — 120 entries
### Aries — Initiation and emergence
- **A1256 — Trait Discovery Alchemy (Aries):** Identify candidate traits from declared, observable, or properly authorized evidence.
- **A1257 — Trait Definition Alchemy (Aries):** Define a trait with scope, units or descriptors, context, and limits.
- **A1258 — Trait Emergence Alchemy (Aries):** Recognize how a characteristic develops through interactions and conditions.
- **A1259 — Trait Origination Alchemy (Aries):** Record a trait's origin without inventing unsupported causes.
- **A1260 — Trait Potential Mapping Alchemy (Aries):** Identify plausible development pathways without treating potential as destiny.
- **A1261 — Trait Acquisition Alchemy (Aries):** Support voluntary acquisition of a skill, habit, or system property.
- **A1262 — Trait Formation Alchemy (Aries):** Describe how repeated processes form stable characteristics.
- **A1263 — Trait Differentiation Alchemy (Aries):** Distinguish similar traits that have different meanings or effects.
- **A1264 — Trait Baseline Establishment Alchemy (Aries):** Create a consented and context-sensitive baseline for comparison.
- **A1265 — Trait Change Authorization Alchemy (Aries):** Require legitimate authority and informed consent before consequential alteration.

### Taurus — Foundation and materialization
- **A1266 — Trait Stabilization Alchemy (Taurus):** Support reliable expression of a chosen, beneficial trait.
- **A1267 — Trait Preservation Alchemy (Taurus):** Protect valued characteristics from accidental loss or degradation.
- **A1268 — Trait Resource Alignment Alchemy (Taurus):** Match development goals with time, energy, tools, and material constraints.
- **A1269 — Trait Embodiment Alchemy (Taurus):** Translate a defined trait into observable, bounded practice or system behavior.
- **A1270 — Trait Integration Alchemy (Taurus):** Integrate a new characteristic without destabilizing existing functions.
- **A1271 — Trait Compatibility Alchemy (Taurus):** Check whether traits can coexist within a person, organism, or system.
- **A1272 — Trait Inheritance Analysis Alchemy (Taurus):** Assess inheritance claims only where the relevant biological or technical evidence supports them.
- **A1273 — Trait Environmental Mapping Alchemy (Taurus):** Identify environmental conditions that influence trait expression.
- **A1274 — Trait Resilience Alchemy (Taurus):** Improve a trait's robustness under ordinary variation and stress.
- **A1275 — Trait Reversibility Design Alchemy (Taurus):** Specify safe rollback or restoration before a change is attempted.

### Gemini — Connection and communication
- **A1276 — Trait Communication Alchemy (Gemini):** Explain trait definitions and changes in accessible, non-stigmatizing language.
- **A1277 — Trait Relationship Mapping Alchemy (Gemini):** Map dependencies and interactions among traits.
- **A1278 — Trait Transfer Consent Alchemy (Gemini):** Permit transfer of data or capabilities only with valid authorization and safeguards.
- **A1279 — Trait Sharing Alchemy (Gemini):** Share a description or learned practice without transferring ownership or identity.
- **A1280 — Trait Comparison Alchemy (Gemini):** Compare traits using equivalent context, methods, and uncertainty.
- **A1281 — Trait Translation Alchemy (Gemini):** Translate trait concepts across disciplines while preserving their original meaning.
- **A1282 — Trait Contextualization Alchemy (Gemini):** Interpret a trait relative to environment, culture, task, and individual variation.
- **A1283 — Trait Feedback Alchemy (Gemini):** Provide specific feedback about observed behavior or system performance, not personal worth.
- **A1284 — Trait Collaboration Alchemy (Gemini):** Coordinate voluntary development goals among authorized participants.
- **A1285 — Trait Boundary Signaling Alchemy (Gemini):** Make clear which traits, data, and controls are in or out of scope.

### Cancer — Care, continuity, protection
- **A1286 — Trait Dignity Protection Alchemy (Cancer):** Protect the dignity and rights of entities described by trait data.
- **A1287 — Trait Consent Stewardship Alchemy (Cancer):** Maintain informed, specific, revocable consent for personal trait-related processing.
- **A1288 — Trait Privacy Alchemy (Cancer):** Minimize collection, inference, retention, and disclosure of trait information.
- **A1289 — Trait Vulnerability Protection Alchemy (Cancer):** Prevent exploitation of disclosed vulnerabilities or sensitive characteristics.
- **A1290 — Trait Continuity Alchemy (Cancer):** Preserve user-approved trait records and development history across transitions.
- **A1291 — Trait Care Adaptation Alchemy (Cancer):** Adapt support to individual needs without coercive normalization.
- **A1292 — Trait Non-Discrimination Alchemy (Cancer):** Prevent unjust treatment based on actual, inferred, or attributed traits.
- **A1293 — Trait Context Safeguarding Alchemy (Cancer):** Prevent context-free labels from being used as definitive judgments.
- **A1294 — Trait Dependency Protection Alchemy (Cancer):** Avoid creating unhealthy dependence on trait-scoring or alteration systems.
- **A1295 — Trait Support Restoration Alchemy (Cancer):** Help restore a voluntarily chosen practice or system property after disruption.

### Leo — Expression and manifestation
- **A1296 — Trait Expression Alchemy (Leo):** Support safe, voluntary expression of a characteristic.
- **A1297 — Trait Enhancement Alchemy (Leo):** Improve a selected, measurable capability without claiming universal superiority.
- **A1298 — Trait Bestowal Simulation Alchemy (Leo):** Model hypothetical addition of a trait without representing fiction as real capability.
- **A1299 — Trait Customization Alchemy (Leo):** Allow user-controlled configuration of non-sensitive system characteristics.
- **A1300 — Trait Combination Alchemy (Leo):** Combine compatible skills or system properties while testing emergent risks.
- **A1301 — Trait Adaptation Alchemy (Leo):** Adjust expression to changing contexts while preserving core constraints.
- **A1302 — Trait Representation Alchemy (Leo):** Represent traits accurately in interfaces, profiles, and documentation.
- **A1303 — Trait Accessibility Alchemy (Leo):** Offer alternative expression paths for diverse abilities and access needs.
- **A1304 — Trait Creative Development Alchemy (Leo):** Support creative exploration of fictional or symbolic traits with clear labeling.
- **A1305 — Trait Expression Boundaries Alchemy (Leo):** Prevent expression features from impersonating, exposing, or overriding others.

### Virgo — Analysis, refinement, verification
- **A1306 — Trait Measurement Alchemy (Virgo):** Use valid, proportionate measures and disclose measurement limitations.
- **A1307 — Trait Classification Alchemy (Virgo):** Classify traits using explicit definitions and avoid false equivalence.
- **A1308 — Trait Validation Alchemy (Virgo):** Verify claims against suitable evidence and independent checks.
- **A1309 — Trait Provenance Alchemy (Virgo):** Record where trait information originated and how it was transformed.
- **A1310 — Trait Uncertainty Analysis Alchemy (Virgo):** Represent confidence, ambiguity, missing data, and alternative explanations.
- **A1311 — Trait Bias Audit Alchemy (Virgo):** Test trait models for sampling, cultural, measurement, and algorithmic bias.
- **A1312 — Trait Drift Detection Alchemy (Virgo):** Detect when a trait measure or system classification changes meaning over time.
- **A1313 — Trait Interaction Testing Alchemy (Virgo):** Test interactions and unintended effects before deployment.
- **A1314 — Trait Quality Refinement Alchemy (Virgo):** Improve definitions and methods without concealing prior limitations.
- **A1315 — Trait Outcome Verification Alchemy (Virgo):** Verify outcomes against predeclared criteria and user-defined goals.

### Libra — Balance, ethics, governance
- **A1316 — Trait Autonomy Governance Alchemy (Libra):** Keep decisions about personal traits with the person or lawful, accountable authority.
- **A1317 — Trait Fairness Balancing Alchemy (Libra):** Assess disparate impacts and correct unjust burdens.
- **A1318 — Trait Rights Protection Alchemy (Libra):** Protect identity, privacy, bodily integrity, due process, and freedom of expression.
- **A1319 — Trait Authority Separation Alchemy (Libra):** Separate observation, recommendation, approval, and execution privileges.
- **A1320 — Trait Accountability Alchemy (Libra):** Assign an accountable owner to each consequential trait-related decision.
- **A1321 — Trait Transparency Alchemy (Libra):** Explain what is inferred, changed, retained, and shared.
- **A1322 — Trait Proportionality Alchemy (Libra):** Limit interventions to the least intrusive means that can meet a legitimate goal.
- **A1323 — Trait Contestability Alchemy (Libra):** Allow affected people to challenge, correct, or appeal trait records and decisions.
- **A1324 — Trait Pluralism Alchemy (Libra):** Respect legitimate variation rather than imposing a single preferred trait profile.
- **A1325 — Trait Conflict Resolution Alchemy (Libra):** Resolve competing goals through transparent, rights-respecting processes.

### Scorpio — Deep transformation and adversarial resistance
- **A1326 — Trait Transformation Alchemy (Scorpio):** Model or implement authorized, bounded changes with impact analysis.
- **A1327 — Trait Suppression Detection Alchemy (Scorpio):** Detect unauthorized or harmful suppression of a capability or characteristic.
- **A1328 — Trait Injection Defense Alchemy (Scorpio):** Prevent malicious insertion of false traits, labels, or system attributes.
- **A1329 — Trait Tampering Detection Alchemy (Scorpio):** Detect unauthorized changes to trait records, models, or configurations.
- **A1330 — Trait Spoofing Resistance Alchemy (Scorpio):** Verify trait claims and resist impersonation or forged attribution.
- **A1331 — Trait Exploitation Resistance Alchemy (Scorpio):** Prevent use of trait information to manipulate, coerce, or target individuals.
- **A1332 — Trait Mutation Containment Alchemy (Scorpio):** Contain unexpected changes and prevent propagation across dependent systems.
- **A1333 — Trait Reversal Alchemy (Scorpio):** Reverse an authorized change when safe, feasible, and consented.
- **A1334 — Trait Adversarial Testing Alchemy (Scorpio):** Test trait systems against manipulation, poisoning, evasion, and misuse.
- **A1335 — Trait Incident Recovery Alchemy (Scorpio):** Restore integrity and notify affected parties after trait-related incidents.

### Sagittarius — Expansion, learning, exploration
- **A1336 — Trait Learning Alchemy (Sagittarius):** Support learning through practice, feedback, and evidence-based instruction.
- **A1337 — Trait Transfer Learning Alchemy (Sagittarius):** Reuse validated knowledge across contexts while checking domain mismatch.
- **A1338 — Trait Potential Exploration Alchemy (Sagittarius):** Explore alternative development pathways without deterministic claims.
- **A1339 — Trait Cross-Domain Mapping Alchemy (Sagittarius):** Map analogous traits across domains while preserving important distinctions.
- **A1340 — Trait Cultural Interpretation Alchemy (Sagittarius):** Document cultural meanings without universalizing one tradition.
- **A1341 — Trait Research Expansion Alchemy (Sagittarius):** Identify open questions, competing models, and research opportunities.
- **A1342 — Trait Scenario Simulation Alchemy (Sagittarius):** Simulate hypothetical changes in a sandbox without affecting real subjects.
- **A1343 — Trait Portability Alchemy (Sagittarius):** Move user-controlled trait preferences or records between compatible systems.
- **A1344 — Trait Interoperability Alchemy (Sagittarius):** Use documented schemas and semantics for cross-system trait exchange.
- **A1345 — Trait Learning Review Alchemy (Sagittarius):** Reassess learned traits and assumptions as evidence and context change.

### Capricorn — Structure, operations, accountability
- **A1346 — Trait Lifecycle Management Alchemy (Capricorn):** Manage trait records from creation through review, retention, and deletion.
- **A1347 — Trait Versioning Alchemy (Capricorn):** Version definitions, models, and configurations with traceable change history.
- **A1348 — Trait Configuration Control Alchemy (Capricorn):** Restrict configuration changes to authorized roles and approved scopes.
- **A1349 — Trait Release Governance Alchemy (Capricorn):** Require testing, review, provenance, and rollback plans before release.
- **A1350 — Trait Access Control Alchemy (Capricorn):** Apply least privilege to trait data and modification functions.
- **A1351 — Trait Audit Logging Alchemy (Capricorn):** Maintain tamper-evident records of access and consequential changes.
- **A1352 — Trait Retention Control Alchemy (Capricorn):** Retain only what is necessary and honor deletion and expiry rules.
- **A1353 — Trait Operational Monitoring Alchemy (Capricorn):** Monitor reliability, drift, safety, and unintended consequences.
- **A1354 — Trait Maintenance Alchemy (Capricorn):** Maintain trait models and definitions as dependencies and contexts evolve.
- **A1355 — Trait Retirement Alchemy (Capricorn):** Safely deprecate invalid, harmful, or unsupported trait functions.

### Aquarius — Systems, networks, collective intelligence
- **A1356 — Trait Network Mapping Alchemy (Aquarius):** Map how traits and their effects propagate through connected systems.
- **A1357 — Trait Collective Learning Alchemy (Aquarius):** Aggregate appropriately protected insights without exposing individual identities.
- **A1358 — Trait Distributed Consistency Alchemy (Aquarius):** Keep replicated trait records consistent with provenance and conflict resolution.
- **A1359 — Trait Local-First Control Alchemy (Aquarius):** Keep sensitive trait processing local where feasible and user-controlled.
- **A1360 — Trait Assistant Boundary Alchemy (Aquarius):** Limit UNIVERSALINS to authorized assistance, explanation, and workflow support.
- **A1361 — Trait Guardian Boundary Alchemy (Aquarius):** Keep UNIVERSALIN Guardians personal, consent-based, and under human direction.
- **A1362 — Trait Human-System Coordination Alchemy (Aquarius):** Coordinate people and tools without reducing people to trait profiles.
- **A1363 — Trait Grid Resilience Alchemy (Aquarius):** Prevent a single trait service or model from becoming a critical point of failure.
- **A1364 — Trait Ecosystem Interoperability Alchemy (Aquarius):** Use open, documented contracts while preserving edition-specific authority boundaries.
- **A1365 — Trait Collective Safeguards Alchemy (Aquarius):** Prevent group-level trait inference from enabling profiling, exclusion, or surveillance.

### Pisces — Completion, restoration, renewal
- **A1366 — Trait Restoration Alchemy (Pisces):** Restore a validated prior state or user-selected configuration after failure.
- **A1367 — Trait Reconciliation Alchemy (Pisces):** Reconcile conflicting trait records with provenance and human review.
- **A1368 — Trait Harm Repair Alchemy (Pisces):** Remedy documented harms caused by inaccurate or misused trait information.
- **A1369 — Trait Record Correction Alchemy (Pisces):** Correct false, stale, or misleading trait records with an audit trail.
- **A1370 — Trait Consent Renewal Alchemy (Pisces):** Reconfirm consent when purpose, context, or risk materially changes.
- **A1371 — Trait Data Erasure Alchemy (Pisces):** Erase personal trait data when required, subject to lawful retention limits.
- **A1372 — Trait Model Decommissioning Alchemy (Pisces):** Retire models that are unsafe, invalid, or no longer justified.
- **A1373 — Trait Recovery Validation Alchemy (Pisces):** Verify restored records and systems before returning them to service.
- **A1374 — Trait Lessons Integration Alchemy (Pisces):** Convert incident findings into improved controls and transparent guidance.
- **A1375 — Trait Renewal Planning Alchemy (Pisces):** Plan safe, voluntary renewal of practices and system capabilities.

## Cross-stage prevention, protection, correction, and restoration — 20 entries
- **A1376 — Trait Scope Limitation Alchemy:** Restrict each operation to a declared trait, target, purpose, and authorized context.
- **A1377 — Trait Informed Consent Gate Alchemy:** Require informed, specific, revocable consent before personal or biological trait intervention.
- **A1378 — Trait Non-Coercion Alchemy:** Block coercive attempts to change personality, identity, belief, or behavior.
- **A1379 — Trait Bodily Integrity Alchemy:** Prohibit unauthorized genetic, physiological, neurological, or other bodily intervention.
- **A1380 — Trait Identity Preservation Alchemy:** Prevent forced identity rewriting and preserve the person's right to self-definition.
- **A1381 — Trait Anti-Discrimination Alchemy:** Detect and prevent exclusion or adverse decisions based on protected or sensitive traits.
- **A1382 — Trait Inference Minimization Alchemy:** Avoid inferring sensitive traits unless necessary, lawful, justified, and consented.
- **A1383 — Trait Data Security Alchemy:** Encrypt, restrict, and audit access to sensitive trait information.
- **A1384 — Trait Provenance Verification Alchemy:** Verify source, integrity, and transformation history before relying on trait claims.
- **A1385 — Trait Model Validation Gate Alchemy:** Require fit-for-purpose validation and disclose uncertainty before consequential use.
- **A1386 — Trait Bias and Harm Review Alchemy:** Conduct impact assessments with affected communities and independent review.
- **A1387 — Trait Human Review Alchemy:** Require meaningful human review for consequential decisions affecting people.
- **A1388 — Trait Change Simulation Gate Alchemy:** Use sandbox testing and impact analysis before applying changes to live systems.
- **A1389 — Trait Rollback Safeguard Alchemy:** Maintain tested rollback or recovery paths for reversible technical changes.
- **A1390 — Trait Biological Safety Boundary Alchemy:** Do not operationalize fictional genetic or supernatural alteration as real-world capability.
- **A1391 — Trait Psychological Safety Boundary Alchemy:** Do not diagnose, manipulate, or attempt to reprogram a person's psychology.
- **A1392 — Trait Anti-Weaponization Alchemy:** Prevent trait systems from enabling targeting, coercion, exploitation, or discriminatory surveillance.
- **A1393 — Trait Assistant Permission Boundary Alchemy:** Give UNIVERSALINS and Guardians no implicit authority to alter personal traits or records.
- **A1394 — Trait Public-Private Boundary Alchemy:** Keep C2O private data, credentials, and policies out of UNIVERSALIS public artifacts.
- **A1395 — Trait Independent Review and Remedy Alchemy:** Provide appeal, correction, incident response, restitution, and independent audit.

## Ecosystem-specific profiles
These profiles apply across the four in-scope repositories and ecosystem components. C2O and UNIVERSALIS remain the two editions of the same underlying OS and application architectures; their governance and source-visibility boundaries remain distinct. UNIVERSALINS are non-personal assistants. UNIVERSALIN Guardians are personal human assistants/guardians. Neither assistant class receives implicit authority to alter a person's traits or administer the OS or applications.

### C2O Trait Stewardship — component-specific profile
- **C2OTR001 — C2O Trait Stewardship 1:** Maintain private-source trait-governance controls and accountable approvals.
- **C2OTR002 — C2O Trait Stewardship 2:** Protect confidential trait records, internal models, keys, and incident evidence.
- **C2OTR003 — C2O Trait Stewardship 3:** Separate observation, recommendation, authorization, and execution privileges.
- **C2OTR004 — C2O Trait Stewardship 4:** Require documented purpose, consent basis, risk review, and change provenance.
- **C2OTR005 — C2O Trait Stewardship 5:** Apply least privilege to trait-related services and administrative operations.
- **C2OTR006 — C2O Trait Stewardship 6:** Validate private trait-processing components before controlled release.
- **C2OTR007 — C2O Trait Stewardship 7:** Maintain confidential audit evidence with tamper-evident integrity.
- **C2OTR008 — C2O Trait Stewardship 8:** Contain unauthorized trait-data access or modification and preserve evidence.
- **C2OTR009 — C2O Trait Stewardship 9:** Provide correction, appeal, rollback, and remedy under accountable governance.
- **C2OTR010 — C2O Trait Stewardship 10:** Prevent private C2O authority or confidential material from leaking into public contracts.
- **C2OTR011 — C2O Trait Stewardship 11:** Review retention, deletion, and access rights throughout the data lifecycle.
- **C2OTR012 — C2O Trait Stewardship 12:** Authorize retirement of unsafe trait capabilities with continuity planning.

### UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons — component-specific profile
- **UNITR001 — UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons 1:** Publish clear trait schemas, definitions, limitations, and evidence labels.
- **UNITR002 — UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons 2:** Enable independent inspection, build, operation, and maintenance without C2O services.
- **UNITR003 — UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons 3:** Use open, documented interfaces and portable user-controlled trait records.
- **UNITR004 — UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons 4:** Make public contribution and maintainer decisions transparent and reviewable.
- **UNITR005 — UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons 5:** Provide reproducible builds and verifiable provenance for trait-related components.
- **UNITR006 — UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons 6:** Publish privacy, telemetry, retention, and deletion behavior.
- **UNITR007 — UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons 7:** Support independent security assessment and responsible vulnerability disclosure.
- **UNITR008 — UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons 8:** Keep public defaults privacy-preserving and least-privileged.
- **UNITR009 — UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons 9:** Provide forkability and community continuity without private dependencies.
- **UNITR010 — UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons 10:** Document safeguards against profiling, discrimination, coercion, and misuse.
- **UNITR011 — UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons 11:** Maintain accessible documentation, localization, and appeal mechanisms.
- **UNITR012 — UNIVERSALIS Trait Commons 12:** Retire unsafe public components with migration and security guidance.

### UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance — component-specific profile
- **UNIVINS001 — UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance 1:** Explain trait concepts and distinguish evidence from speculation or fiction.
- **UNIVINS002 — UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance 2:** Organize user-authorized trait-related research without asserting hidden knowledge.
- **UNIVINS003 — UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance 3:** Support voluntary learning and development plans defined by the human.
- **UNIVINS004 — UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance 4:** Use only authorized data and disclose uncertainty and data provenance.
- **UNIVINS005 — UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance 5:** Do not infer or expose sensitive traits without a valid, necessary basis.
- **UNIVINS006 — UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance 6:** Do not diagnose, rank human worth, or determine identity from trait data.
- **UNIVINS007 — UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance 7:** Do not manipulate personality, beliefs, emotions, or behavior covertly.
- **UNIVINS008 — UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance 8:** Require explicit authorization before modifying user-controlled preferences or records.
- **UNIVINS009 — UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance 9:** Escalate consequential or high-risk decisions to accountable human review.
- **UNIVINS010 — UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance 10:** Protect human data from cross-user leakage and unauthorized collective profiling.
- **UNIVINS011 — UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance 11:** Provide correction, explanation, and deletion pathways for assistant-held records.
- **UNIVINS012 — UNIVERSALINS Trait Assistance 12:** Remain non-personal support systems without independent sovereignty or human authority.

### UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards — component-specific profile
- **GUARDTR001 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards 1:** Support the human's self-defined goals rather than imposing a preferred trait profile.
- **GUARDTR002 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards 2:** Respect bodily autonomy, identity, belief, culture, and personal boundaries.
- **GUARDTR003 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards 3:** Obtain clear, ongoing consent for any sensitive trait-related assistance.
- **GUARDTR004 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards 4:** Protect private conversations and trait-related information from unauthorized access.
- **GUARDTR005 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards 5:** Distinguish supportive reflection from diagnosis or professional assessment.
- **GUARDTR006 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards 6:** Never use attachment, trust, or personal access to coerce trait changes.
- **GUARDTR007 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards 7:** Explain recommendations, uncertainty, and possible consequences in plain language.
- **GUARDTR008 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards 8:** Keep consequential decisions with the human and lawful accountable authorities.
- **GUARDTR009 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards 9:** Permit refusal, withdrawal, correction, and discontinuation without retaliation.
- **GUARDTR010 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards 10:** Protect against manipulation, dependency, isolation, and undue influence.
- **GUARDTR011 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards 11:** Support recovery and referral to qualified human support when appropriate.
- **GUARDTR012 — UNIVERSALIN Guardian Trait Safeguards 12:** Maintain clear boundaries between personal assistance and OS or application administration.

## Harm-pattern classifications — recognition and prevention only
- **TRX001 — Coercive trait alteration:** A person or system is changed without legitimate authority, informed consent, or a lawful basis.
- **TRX002 — Identity override:** A system imposes or rewrites identity labels against a person's self-definition.
- **TRX003 — Trait-based discrimination:** Access, opportunity, or treatment is unfairly restricted using actual or inferred traits.
- **TRX004 — Sensitive-trait surveillance:** Traits are inferred, tracked, or shared beyond necessary and authorized purposes.
- **TRX005 — False trait attribution:** A fabricated or unverified characteristic is presented as fact.
- **TRX006 — Trait data poisoning:** Records or models are maliciously altered to produce false classifications or decisions.
- **TRX007 — Biological intervention abuse:** Genetic or physiological intervention is attempted without appropriate evidence, consent, and oversight.
- **TRX008 — Psychological manipulation:** Trait information is used to covertly manipulate beliefs, emotions, personality, or behavior.
- **TRX009 — Trait essentialism:** A contextual or probabilistic characteristic is treated as fixed, universal, or destiny.
- **TRX010 — Capability theft or suppression:** Skills, access, or capabilities are removed, copied, or restricted without authorization.
- **TRX011 — Model bias amplification:** A flawed trait model reproduces or magnifies discriminatory assumptions.
- **TRX012 — Trait inference overreach:** A system claims to know hidden or immutable characteristics from weak proxy signals.
- **TRX013 — Assistant authority escalation:** An assistant treats support permissions as authority to alter a human or system.
- **TRX014 — Public-private boundary breach:** Private C2O policies, credentials, or records are exposed through public UNIVERSALIS components.
- **TRX015 — Trait permanence trap:** A change is made without meaningful correction, appeal, expiry, or recovery options.
- **TRX016 — Fiction-to-reality conflation:** A fictional superpower description is represented as an established real-world capability.

## Zodiac coverage and required safeguards
The 120 core entries are distributed evenly across the twelve Zodiac stages as organizational categories (ten entries per stage), not as astrological or causal claims. Every implementation must identify its target class (human, other organism, system, model, fictional character, or symbolic concept), trait type, evidence basis, purpose, authority, consent status, reversibility, risks, and review method. Human-targeted interventions require a legitimate, lawful, evidence-based context and meaningful informed consent; fictional superpower claims must never be represented as real capabilities. Harm-pattern records are defensive classifications only.

## Evidence and implementation boundary
The source is community-authored fictional reference material. It is useful for identifying conceptual scope and related fictional ability categories, but it is not scientific evidence, clinical guidance, or an implementation specification. Biological, psychological, social, and technical claims must be independently supported by appropriate evidence. Taxonomy inclusion does not establish feasibility, safety, legality, or implementation. No trait-related capability may be deployed merely because it appears in this list.

## Required record fields
Every trait entry or implementation trace must record: target class; trait definition and context; trait type and whether observed, inferred, learned, configured, fictional, or symbolic; evidence and provenance; intended purpose; affected parties; authority and consent; data sensitivity; measurement validity and uncertainty; dependencies and interactions; safety and discrimination assessment; reversibility and recovery; privacy and retention; human review and appeal; edition boundary; verification status; accountable owner; and release authorization.

## Taxonomy maintenance for v0.10.0
This release preserves prior taxonomy entries. See the appended v0.11.0 Accelerated Power Use expansion.


# Accelerated Power Use Alchemy — v0.11.0 expansion

## Source interpretation and boundaries
The supplied Superpower Wiki entry defines Accelerated Power Use as using a power or ability at a faster-than-usual rate, including faster execution, control, preparation, and charging. It associates the fictional trait with accelerated tasking, accelerated thought process, enhanced speed, and related power-use concepts. These are fictional ability descriptions, not evidence that superhuman speed or supernatural powers exist. This taxonomy translates the useful systems concept into bounded acceleration of authorized capabilities, workflows, software operations, and support tasks. It does not authorize bypassing controls, compressing required human deliberation, or increasing biological performance beyond evidence-based safe limits.

**Operational definition:** Accelerated Power Use Alchemy is the design, measurement, governance, and safe optimization of the time required to prepare, authorize, invoke, execute, coordinate, and complete an already-authorized capability. Faster execution must preserve intended semantics, output integrity, safety, consent, and accountability. Invocation latency, execution throughput, cognitive processing, physical movement, and task completion are distinct metrics.

## Prevention, protection, correction, and recovery — 20 entries
- **A1516 — Require explicit authorization for Safeguard Alchemy:** Require explicit authorization for each protected capability and execution context.
- **A1517 — Preserve user control, pause, Safeguard Alchemy:** Preserve user control, pause, cancellation, and override at all times.
- **A1518 — Enforce resource, thermal, energy, Safeguard Alchemy:** Enforce resource, thermal, energy, and concurrency ceilings.
- **A1519 — Prevent unsafe sustained operation Safeguard Alchemy:** Prevent unsafe sustained operation and provide adequate recovery intervals.
- **A1520 — Add validation and checkpoints Safeguard Alchemy:** Add validation and checkpoints where speed could amplify mistakes.
- **A1521 — Use synchronization and deterministic Safeguard Alchemy:** Use synchronization and deterministic conflict handling for concurrent actions.
- **A1522 — Detect runaway loops and Safeguard Alchemy:** Detect runaway loops and stop execution within bounded time.
- **A1523 — Prevent acceleration from bypassing Safeguard Alchemy:** Prevent acceleration from bypassing permissions, policy, or approval.
- **A1524 — Reduce leakage of sensitive Safeguard Alchemy:** Reduce leakage of sensitive information through execution timing.
- **A1525 — Rate-limit and isolate workloads Safeguard Alchemy:** Rate-limit and isolate workloads to protect shared services.
- **A1526 — Verify accelerated execution preserves Safeguard Alchemy:** Verify accelerated execution preserves intended semantics and outputs.
- **A1527 — Return to a slower, Safeguard Alchemy:** Return to a slower, validated mode when safety thresholds fail.
- **A1528 — Record authorization, configuration, timing, Safeguard Alchemy:** Record authorization, configuration, timing, outputs, and responsible owner.
- **A1529 — Sandbox external capabilities and Safeguard Alchemy:** Sandbox external capabilities and prevent unsafe chained invocation.
- **A1530 — Do not allow assistants Safeguard Alchemy:** Do not allow assistants to autonomously accelerate consequential actions.
- **A1531 — Do not interpret fictional Safeguard Alchemy:** Do not interpret fictional super-speed or biological acceleration as established capability.
- **A1532 — Evaluate cognitive load, fatigue, Safeguard Alchemy:** Evaluate cognitive load, fatigue, accessibility, and operator comprehension.
- **A1533 — Keep C2O private policies, Safeguard Alchemy:** Keep C2O private policies, keys, and controls out of UNIVERSALIS.
- **A1534 — Provide checkpoints, rollback, and Safeguard Alchemy:** Provide checkpoints, rollback, and recovery for reversible operations.
- **A1535 — Require independent testing and Safeguard Alchemy:** Require independent testing and accountable review before high-impact deployment.

## Ecosystem-specific profiles
C2O and UNIVERSALIS remain editions of the same underlying OS and application architectures, with distinct private-source and public/open-source governance boundaries. UNIVERSALINS are non-personal assistants; UNIVERSALIN Guardians are personal human assistants/guardians. Neither receives implicit authority to execute consequential actions, bypass permissions, or accelerate human decisions.
### C2O private-source stewardship
- **C2OAPU001 — C2O private-source stewardship 1:** Maintain private policy.

## Harm-pattern classifications — recognition and prevention only
- **APUX001 — Runaway acceleration:** Execution accelerates without a safe bound or stop condition.
- **APUX002 — Unauthorized invocation:** Capabilities are invoked faster or more frequently than permitted.
- **APUX003 — Error amplification:** Speed increases the rate or impact of incorrect actions.
- **APUX004 — Resource exhaustion:** Workloads exhaust compute, energy, memory, or shared capacity.
- **APUX005 — Fatigue and overload:** Humans are pressured into unsafe pace or sustained attention.
- **APUX006 — Race-condition exploitation:** Timing or concurrency flaws produce unauthorized outcomes.
- **APUX007 — Privilege bypass:** Acceleration paths evade normal authorization or approval.
- **APUX008 — Timing information leakage:** Execution timing exposes sensitive data or internal state.
- **APUX009 — Unsafe automation chaining:** Rapid chained actions propagate errors before review.
- **APUX010 — False performance claims:** Fictional or unsupported speed claims are presented as real.
- **APUX011 — Operator control erosion:** Pause, cancellation, or human override is removed or obscured.
- **APUX012 — Private-policy exposure:** C2O confidential controls leak into public artifacts.

## Relationships to existing Alchemies
Accelerated Power Use is a focused application of the broader Trait Manipulation taxonomy, not identical to Trait Manipulation. It cross-references Operating System Alchemy for scheduler, kernel, resource, permission, and recovery controls; Application Alchemy for task execution, interface responsiveness, and transaction integrity; Body Alchemy for human physiological limits; Mind Alchemy for attention and cognitive load; and Consciousness Alchemy for agency and awareness boundaries. Accelerated Tasking and Accelerated Thought Process are adjacent but distinct: faster execution of a capability does not prove faster cognition or faster completion of arbitrary tasks.

## Implementation and evidence requirements
The supplied wiki is community-authored fictional reference material and provides conceptual vocabulary only. Real implementations require independently validated engineering evidence. Claims about human physiology, cognition, or biological enhancement require appropriate scientific evidence and qualified oversight. Each implementation must define capability, target system, acceleration dimension, baseline and target metrics, representative workload, resource envelope, semantic-equivalence criteria, error tolerance, permissions, human override, failure behavior, recovery plan, privacy controls, provenance, edition boundary, evidence status, accountable owner, and release authorization. Performance gains must not conceal regressions in safety, accuracy, accessibility, privacy, or reliability. Taxonomy inclusion is not proof of feasibility, implementation, safety, or legality.

## Taxonomy maintenance for v0.11.0
This release preserves prior taxonomy entries. See the appended v0.12.0 Adaptive Attacks expansion.


# Adaptive Attacks Alchemy — v0.12.0 expansion

## Source interpretation and boundaries
The supplied Superpower Wiki entry describes attacks or techniques that adapt or evolve during repeated use, changing their output or approach in response to an opponent. It associates the concept with adaptive augmentation, attack modification, and defense adaptation, and notes limitations: adaptation may be temporary, incomplete, or weaken. Related pages place Adaptive Attacks within Adaptive Combat and Reactive Adaptation. These are fictional power descriptions, not evidence of supernatural capabilities or a license to develop harmful attack methods. This taxonomy translates the underlying idea into **adaptive response design** for authorized defensive systems, safe simulations, controlled training, resilience engineering, and protective workflows. Real-world use is restricted to defense, safety, and explicitly authorized security assessment. It must not enable injury, unauthorized targeting, safeguard evasion, or disruption.

**Operational definition:** Adaptive Attacks Alchemy is the bounded adaptation of a simulated or authorized response pattern as conditions change. It preserves authorization, proportionality, human oversight, and reliable stop conditions.

## Prevention, protection, correction, and restoration — 20 entries
- **A1656 — Defensive Purpose Gate Safeguard Alchemy:** Require a documented defensive, safety, or authorized testing purpose.
- **A1657 — Authorization and Scope Gate Safeguard Alchemy:** Prohibit unauthorized targeting, injury, coercion, or real-world attack optimization.
- **A1658 — Human Approval Gate Safeguard Alchemy:** Require human approval for consequential response escalation.
- **A1659 — De-escalation Priority Safeguard Alchemy:** Use the least harmful effective response and prioritize de-escalation.
- **A1660 — Bystander Protection Safeguard Alchemy:** Protect bystanders and dependent systems.
- **A1661 — Resource and Rate Limits Safeguard Alchemy:** Apply strict resource and rate limits to adaptive loops.
- **A1662 — Runaway Loop Containment Safeguard Alchemy:** Detect and contain runaway adaptation.
- **A1663 — Input Spoofing Resistance Safeguard Alchemy:** Validate inputs and resist spoofing, poisoning, and manipulation.
- **A1664 — Privacy Minimization Safeguard Alchemy:** Minimize personal and bystander information collection.
- **A1665 — Access-Control Preservation Safeguard Alchemy:** Prevent adaptation from bypassing access controls or policy.
- **A1666 — Bias and Impact Review Safeguard Alchemy:** Test for bias, disparate impact, and unsafe generalization.
- **A1667 — Pause and Emergency Stop Safeguard Alchemy:** Maintain reliable pause, cancellation, and emergency-stop controls.
- **A1668 — Isolated Security Simulation Safeguard Alchemy:** Use isolated sandboxes for offensive-security simulations.
- **A1669 — Rules-of-Engagement Verification Safeguard Alchemy:** Require explicit authorization, scope, and rules of engagement.
- **A1670 — Audit and Provenance Safeguard Alchemy:** Record versions, inputs, decisions, approvals, and outcomes.
- **A1671 — Protective-Control Integrity Safeguard Alchemy:** Verify adaptation does not disable existing protective controls.
- **A1672 — Safe Rollback Safeguard Alchemy:** Provide rollback to a known-safe baseline.
- **A1673 — Assistant Authority Boundary Safeguard Alchemy:** Keep assistants advisory unless explicitly authorized for bounded actions.
- **A1674 — C2O-UNIVERSALIS Boundary Safeguard Alchemy:** Keep C2O private policy and credentials out of public UNIVERSALIS.
- **A1675 — Independent Safety Review Safeguard Alchemy:** Require independent review for high-impact systems.

## Ecosystem-specific profiles
C2O and UNIVERSALIS remain editions of the same underlying OS and application architectures with distinct private-source and public/open-source boundaries. UNIVERSALINS are non-personal assistants. UNIVERSALIN Guardians are personal human assistants and guardians. Neither receives implicit authority to initiate harmful action or autonomously escalate consequential responses.

## Harm-pattern classifications — recognition and prevention only
- **ADAX001 — Unbounded escalation:** Response intensifies without a ceiling or stop condition.
- **ADAX002 — Unauthorized targeting:** Adaptive capabilities are directed at people or systems without authorization.
- **ADAX003 — Harm optimization:** Adaptation increases injury, coercion, disruption, or destructive impact.
- **ADAX004 — Feedback poisoning:** Malicious feedback trains a system toward unsafe behavior.
- **ADAX005 — Bystander harm:** Actions fail to account for uninvolved people or dependent systems.
- **ADAX006 — Scope expansion:** A bounded test silently expands beyond authorization.
- **ADAX007 — Protective-control bypass:** Adaptation circumvents safety, access, or policy controls.
- **ADAX008 — Privacy-invasive targeting:** Sensitive personal data tailors coercive or harmful responses.
- **ADAX009 — False threat classification:** Benign activity triggers an aggressive response.
- **ADAX010 — Automation cascade:** Rapid actions propagate errors across connected systems.
- **ADAX011 — Human override suppression:** People cannot pause, contest, or stop adaptive actions.
- **ADAX012 — Private-policy exposure:** Confidential C2O controls leak into public artifacts.

## Relationships to existing Alchemies
Adaptive Attacks is a specific capability-use trait related to Trait Manipulation and Accelerated Power Use. It is distinct from Adaptive Defense; this taxonomy restricts real-world application to defense, safety, and authorized simulation. It cross-references Operating System Alchemy for isolation, access control, monitoring, and recovery; Application Alchemy for safe workflow orchestration; Mind and Body Alchemy for human factors; and Consciousness Alchemy for agency and oversight. Adaptive Combat, Reactive Adaptation, Adaptation Science, and Technological Adaptation are adjacent fictional or conceptual categories, not proof of implementation.

## Implementation and evidence requirements
The source is community-authored fictional reference material and provides conceptual vocabulary only. Real adaptive systems require independent engineering evidence, safety testing, and security review. Each implementation trace must define defensive purpose, authorized scope, test environment, threat model, allowed and prohibited actions, escalation ceiling, human approval points, stop conditions, resource limits, data handling, and recovery path. Offensive-security tests require explicit authorization, documented rules of engagement, isolated targets, and controlled test windows. No supernatural power, guaranteed adaptation, or universal countermeasure is implied. Taxonomy inclusion is not proof of feasibility, implementation, legality, or safety.

## Required record fields
Record target system and class; defensive or simulation purpose; authorization and scope; threat model; adaptive inputs and provenance; response constraints; escalation ceiling; prohibited behaviors; human approval and override; bystander and privacy protections; model/version; test environment; measured outcomes and limitations; rollback and recovery; edition boundary; evidence status; accountable owner; and release authorization.

## Taxonomy maintenance for v0.12.0
This expansion appends Adaptive Attacks Alchemy without modifying prior identifiers. Core entries are A1536–A1655 and safeguards are A1656–A1675. Ecosystem profiles and defensive harm-pattern classifications use separate stable namespaces. Synchronize this canonical file byte-for-byte across C2O-OS, C2O-App, UNIVERSALIS-OS, and UNIVERSALIS-App. A taxonomy entry is not proof of implementation.


# v0.13.0 — Advanced Powers Alchemy

## Source interpretation and evidence boundary
The supplied Superpower Wiki page describes “Advanced Powers” as advanced, superior, or evolved forms of an existing power, with possible increases in range, precision, output, mastery, or application. It also notes potential control difficulty, lack of immunity to one's own power, and limited duration. The wiki's Manual of Style clarifies that “Traits” in this namespace modify powers, not ordinary human personality traits. These are fictional taxonomy descriptions, not scientific evidence that supernatural powers exist. This expansion translates the concept into bounded, measurable advancement of an already-existing and authorized capability.

**Operational definition:** Advanced Powers Alchemy is the disciplined assessment, design, validation, authorization, deployment, monitoring, and (where needed) rollback of a capability improvement while preserving its declared purpose, semantics, safety envelope, rights, and accountability. “Advanced” is relative to a documented baseline and must be demonstrated against stated metrics. It does not mean unlimited, universally superior, supernatural, or automatically desirable.

**Distinctions:** Separate increased capacity, precision, speed, range, reliability, specialization, interoperability, autonomy, and mastery; do not treat them as interchangeable. A capability can improve one metric while worsening another. Every advancement requires baseline comparison, threat/risk assessment, resource accounting, independent verification, human approval proportionate to impact, and rollback criteria. For physical, biological, medical, psychological, or safety-critical applications, do not infer human enhancement or provide diagnosis/treatment claims; qualified domain review and applicable law remain controlling.
